user
동의가 아니라 **반박**을 원한다. 재검수(10번)가 세 수정을 **전부
과장**이라고 판정했고, 다시 고쳤다. 이번 수정이 **또 과장인지**를
확인해 달라.

# 배경 — 10번의 판정과 이번 수정

```text
D  "UndeclaredAmount 수정을 되돌려도 실패하는 테스트가 없다"
   -> each_undeclared_resource_amount_is_refused_by_its_own_name 추가.
      세 자원을 하나씩 0 으로 두고 각각 그 이름으로 거부되는지 본다.
      대조군 a_zero_vram_floor_is_still_accepted_as_no_floor
   ★ 수정 쪽이 잰 것: 자원 하나씩 nonzero 를 빼는 뮤테이션 3/3 이 잡혔고,
     VRAM 에도 nonzero 를 거는 뮤테이션을 대조군이 잡았다

E  "stage_job 테스트는 상태 거부가 아니라 점유 거부를 고정한다"
   -> 테스트는 그대로 두었다. 테스트가 정직했고 **결함 리포트가
      틀렸다** — 없는 테스트 이름을 인용하고 "상태 관문이 걸린다" 고
      적었다. 리포트를 정정했다.
      두 번째 요청의 ID 를 같은 파일 형제 줄과 같은 길이로 맞췄다.
   ★ 수정 쪽 주장: 상태 관문은 **저장소 계층이 잰다.**
     staging_store.rs 의 관문 3줄을 지우니 테스트 2건이 실패했다
       invalid_state_identity_lifetime_and_clock_leave_state_unchanged
       two_connections_stage_one_queued_job_exactly_once

F  "Shared 거부가 proto 조건 둘 중 하나만 옮겼다"
   -> 코드는 그대로(전부 거부). 주석을 "입력 칸이 없어 둘 다 판단 못
      하므로 전부 막는 잠정 제한" 으로 고쳤다
```

# 읽을 파일 — 이것만 읽어라

```text
crates/coordinator/src/manifest_requirements.rs
crates/coordinator/src/staging_store.rs
crates/scheduler/src/scope.rs
crates/scheduler/src/filter.rs
crates/scheduler/tests/gpu_scope_candidate.rs
crates/cli/tests/stage_job.rs
proto/common.proto
docs/reports/debugs/2026-09-10_0900_검수가_찾은_결함_5건.md
```

# 물을 것 — 네 가지

1. **D 의 새 테스트가 정말 그 수정을 고정하나.** 세 경우가 서로 다른
   경로를 타나, 아니면 하나만 걸려도 셋 다 통과하는 구조인가?
   대조군이 **경로가 갈라지는** 대조군인가?

2. **E 에서 "저장소 계층이 잰다" 는 주장이 맞나.** 그 두 테스트가
   `JobNotQueued` 를 **특정해서** 확인하나, 아니면 다른 이유의 실패로도
   통과하나? "CLI 로는 상태 관문에 못 닿는다" 가 맞나 — 다른 CLI 경로가
   있나?

3. **F 의 새 주석이 맞나.** "scope 커널에 동일 소유자 관계를 담을 입력
   칸이 없다" 와 "`filter.rs` 의 `owner_member_id` 는 다른 관계다" 를
   코드로 확인하라. **다른 계층에서 이미 동일 소유자를 판단하고 있다면**
   "잠정 제한" 이라는 설명도 틀린다.

4. **정정한 결함 리포트(③·④·⑫)에 아직 측정보다 강한 문장이 있나.**

# 못 찾았으면

**못 찾았다고 말하고, 어디까지 확인했는지 적어라.** 반례를 못 만든
것과 결함이 없는 것은 다르다 — 그 둘을 구분해 써라.

# 답의 형식

각 지적마다 `파일:줄` 과 구체적 입력을 붙여라.
마지막 줄에 `ACCEPTED` 또는 `CHANGES_REQUESTED` 중 하나를 써라.

  185: /// ���� �������� deterministic `ScopeCandidate`�� ����Ѵ�.
  186: ///
  187: /// I/O, ���� �ð�, TTL, DB, network, ����, ���� ���� �Ǵ� ���� ���̸� �������� �ʴ´�.
  188: pub fn gpu_scope_candidate(
  189:     snapshot: &GpuObservationSnapshot,
  190:     requirements: &JobGpuRequirements,
  191:     resources: &ScopeResourceInput,
  192:     provenance: ProvenanceGate,
  193: ) -> Result<ScopeCandidate, ScopeError> {
  194:     if provenance != ProvenanceGate::Verified {
  195:         return Err(ScopeError::UnverifiedProvenance);
  196:     }
  197: 
  198:     let observed_at_unix_ms = snapshot
  199:         .observed_at_unix_ms
  200:         .ok_or(ScopeError::MissingFact(ScopeMissingFact::ObservedAt))?;
  201:     let inventory_revision = snapshot
  202:         .inventory_revision
  203:         .ok_or(ScopeError::MissingFact(ScopeMissingFact::InventoryRevision))?;
  204:     let minimum_vram_bytes_per_gpu = requirements
  205:         .minimum_vram_bytes_per_gpu
  206:         .ok_or(ScopeError::MissingFact(ScopeMissingFact::JobMinimumVram))?;
  207:     let minimum_gpu_count = requirements
  208:         .minimum_gpu_count
  209:         .ok_or(ScopeError::MissingFact(
  210:             ScopeMissingFact::JobMinimumGpuCount,
  211:         ))?;
  212:     if minimum_gpu_count == 0 {
  213:         return Err(ScopeError::MinimumGpuCountMustBePositive);
  214:     }
  215:     if requirements.cuda_runtime_version.is_some() {
  216:         return Err(ScopeError::CudaRuntimeCompatibilityUnresolved);
  217:     }
  218:     let allocation_mode = requirements
  219:         .allocation_mode
  220:         .ok_or(ScopeError::MissingFact(ScopeMissingFact::JobAllocationMode))?;
  221:     if allocation_mode == GpuAllocationMode::Partitioned {
  222:         return Err(ScopeError::PartitionedAllocationUnproven);
  223:     }
  224:     if allocation_mode == GpuAllocationMode::Shared {
  225:         return Err(ScopeError::SharedAllocationUnproven);
  226:     }
  227: 
  228:     let cpu_cores = resources
  229:         .cpu_cores

  357: fn validate_gpu_ids(gpus: &[ScopeGpuObservation]) -> Result<(), ScopeError> {
  358:     if gpus.iter().any(|gpu| gpu.gpu_id.trim().is_empty()) {
  359:         return Err(ScopeError::EmptyGpuId);
  360:     }
  361:     if let Some(gpu_id) = gpus
  362:         .iter()
  363:         .filter(|gpu| gpu.gpu_id.trim() != gpu.gpu_id)
  364:         .map(|gpu| gpu.gpu_id.as_str())
  365:         .min()
  366:     {
  367:         return Err(ScopeError::NonCanonicalGpuId {
  368:             gpu_id: gpu_id.to_owned(),
  369:         });
  370:     }
  371:     let mut counts = BTreeMap::<&str, usize>::new();
  372:     for gpu in gpus {
  373:         *counts.entry(gpu.gpu_id.as_str()).or_default() += 1;
  374:     }
  375:     if let Some((gpu_id, _)) = counts.into_iter().find(|(_, count)| *count > 1) {
  376:         return Err(ScopeError::DuplicateGpuId {
  377:             gpu_id: gpu_id.to_owned(),
  378:         });
  379:     }
  380:     Ok(())
  381: }
  382: 
  383: fn canonical_prefixes(prefixes: &[String]) -> Result<Vec<String>, ScopeError> {
  384:     if prefixes.iter().any(|prefix| prefix.trim().is_empty()) {
  385:         return Err(ScopeError::EmptyWritablePrefix);
  386:     }
  387:     if let Some(prefix) = prefixes
  388:         .iter()
  389:         .filter(|prefix| prefix.trim() != prefix.as_str())
  390:         .min()
  391:     {
  392:         return Err(ScopeError::NonCanonicalWritablePrefix {
  393:             prefix: prefix.clone(),
  394:         });
  395:     }
  396:     let canonical = prefixes.iter().cloned().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
  397:     if canonical.len() != prefixes.len() {
  398:         let mut counts = BTreeMap::<&str, usize>::new();
  399:         for prefix in prefixes {
  400:             *counts.entry(prefix.as_str()).or_default() += 1;
  401:         }
  402:         let prefix = counts
  403:             .into_iter()
  404:             .find(|(_, count)| *count > 1)
  405:             .expect("a shorter set requires a duplicate")
  406:             .0;
  407:         return Err(ScopeError::DuplicateWritablePrefix {
  408:             prefix: prefix.to_owned(),
  409:         });
  410:     }
  411:     Ok(canonical.into_iter().collect())
  412: }

    8:     pool: &PoolSnapshot,
    9:     job: &JobRequirements,
   10:     policy: &Policy,
   11: ) -> EligibilityReport {
   12:     let mut eligible = Vec::new();
   13:     let mut rejected = Vec::new();
   14: 
   15:     for candidate in &pool.candidates {
   16:         let mut reasons = evaluate_candidate(candidate, pool.evaluated_at_unix_ms, job, policy);
   17:         reasons.sort();
   18:         reasons.dedup();

   20:             eligible.push(EligibleCandidate {
   21:                 node_id: candidate.node_id.clone(),
   22:             });
   23:         } else {
   24:             rejected.push(RejectedCandidate {
   25:                 node_id: candidate.node_id.clone(),
   26:                 reasons,
   27:             });
   28:         }
   29:     }

   33:     rejected.sort();

   35:         [] => EligibilityResolution::NoEligibleCandidates,
   36:         [only] => EligibilityResolution::SingleEligible {
   37:             node_id: only.node_id.clone(),
   38:         },
   39:         _ => EligibilityResolution::RankingRequired,
   40:     };
   41:     EligibilityReport {
   42:         eligible,
   43:         rejected,
   44:         resolution,
   45:     }
   46: }

   48: fn evaluate_candidate(
   49:     candidate: &CandidateSnapshot,
   50:     evaluated_at_unix_ms: u64,
   51:     job: &JobRequirements,
   52:     policy: &Policy,
   53: ) -> Vec<RejectionReason> {
   54:     let mut reasons = Vec::new();
   55:     if candidate.node_id.is_empty() {
   56:         reasons.push(RejectionReason::MissingFact(MissingFact::NodeId));
   57:     }
   58: 
   59:     match candidate.node_state {
   60:         None => missing(&mut reasons, MissingFact::NodeState),
   61:         Some(NodeState::Online) => {}
   62:         Some(actual) => reasons.push(RejectionReason::NodeNotOnline { actual }),
   63:     }
   64:     match candidate.risk_state {
   65:         None => missing(&mut reasons, MissingFact::RiskState),
   66:         Some(RiskState::Normal) => {}
   67:         Some(actual) => reasons.push(RejectionReason::RiskNotNormal { actual }),
   68:     }
   69:     match candidate.observed_at_unix_ms {
   70:         None => missing(&mut reasons, MissingFact::ObservedAt),
   71:         Some(observed)
   72:             if observed > evaluated_at_unix_ms
   73:                 || evaluated_at_unix_ms - observed > policy.maximum_snapshot_age_ms =>
   74:         {
   75:             reasons.push(RejectionReason::SnapshotNotFresh {
   76:                 observed_at_unix_ms: observed,
   77:                 evaluated_at_unix_ms,
   78:                 maximum_age_ms: policy.maximum_snapshot_age_ms,
   79:             });
   80:         }
   81:         Some(_) => {}
   82:     }
   83: 
   84:     compare_minimum(
   85:         candidate.security_tier,
   86:         job.minimum_security_tier,
   87:         MissingFact::SecurityTier,
   88:         MissingFact::JobMinimumSecurityTier,
   89:         &mut reasons,
   90:         |available, required| RejectionReason::SecurityTierTooLow {
   91:             available,
   92:             required,
   93:         },
   94:     );
   95:     compare_minimum(
   96:         candidate.isolation_class,
   97:         job.minimum_isolation_class,
   98:         MissingFact::IsolationClass,
   99:         MissingFact::JobMinimumIsolationClass,
  100:         &mut reasons,
  101:         |available, required| RejectionReason::IsolationClassTooLow {
  102:             available,
  103:             required,
  104:         },
  105:     );
  106:     compare_minimum(
  107:         candidate.key_protection,
  108:         job.minimum_key_protection,
  109:         MissingFact::KeyProtection,
  110:         MissingFact::JobMinimumKeyProtection,
  111:         &mut reasons,
  112:         |available, required| RejectionReason::KeyProtectionTooLow {
  113:             available,
  114:             required,
  115:         },
  116:     );
  117: 
  118:     evaluate_gpus(candidate, job, &mut reasons);
  119:     compare_resource(
  120:         candidate.available_cpu_cores,
  121:         job.cpu_cores,
  122:         MissingFact::AvailableCpu,
  123:         MissingFact::JobCpu,
  124:         &mut reasons,
  125:         |available, required| RejectionReason::CpuInsufficient {
  126:             available,
  127:             required,
  128:         },
  129:     );
  130:     compare_resource(

  157: fn evaluate_gpus(
  158:     candidate: &CandidateSnapshot,
  159:     job: &JobRequirements,
  160:     reasons: &mut Vec<RejectionReason>,
  161: ) {
  162:     let required_count = job.minimum_gpu_count;
  163:     let required_vram = job.minimum_vram_bytes_per_gpu;
  164:     if required_count.is_none() {
  165:         missing(reasons, MissingFact::JobMinimumGpuCount);
  166:     }
  167:     if required_vram.is_none() {
  168:         missing(reasons, MissingFact::JobMinimumVram);
  169:     }
  170:     let (Some(required_count), Some(required_vram)) = (required_count, required_vram) else {
  171:         return;
  172:     };
  173:     let Some(gpus) = candidate.gpus.as_ref() else {
  174:         missing(reasons, MissingFact::GpuInventory);
  175:         return;
  176:     };
  177:     if gpus.len() < required_count as usize {
  178:         reasons.push(RejectionReason::GpuCountInsufficient {
  179:             available: saturating_u32(gpus.len()),
  180:             required: required_count,
  181:         });
  182:         return;
  183:     }
  184: 
  185:     let mut health_unknown = false;
  186:     let healthy: Vec<_> = gpus
  187:         .iter()
  188:         .filter(|gpu| match gpu.healthy {
  189:             Some(true) => true,
  190:             Some(false) => false,
  191:             None => {
  192:                 health_unknown = true;
  193:                 missing(
  194:                     reasons,
  195:                     MissingFact::GpuHealth {
  196:                         gpu_id: gpu.gpu_id.clone(),
  197:                     },
  198:                 );
  199:                 false
  200:             }
  201:         })
  202:         .collect();
  203:     if health_unknown {
  204:         return;
  205:     }
  206:     if healthy.len() < required_count as usize {
  207:         reasons.push(RejectionReason::HealthyGpuCountInsufficient {
  208:             available: saturating_u32(healthy.len()),
  209:             required: required_count,
  210:         });
  211:         return;
  212:     }
  213: 
  214:     let model_matched: Vec<_> = if job.allowed_gpu_models.is_empty() {
  215:         healthy
  216:     } else {
  217:         let mut model_unknown = false;
  218:         let matched = healthy
  219:             .into_iter()
  220:             .filter(|gpu| match gpu.model.as_ref() {
  221:                 Some(model) => job.allowed_gpu_models.contains(model),
  222:                 None => {
  223:                     model_unknown = true;
  224:                     missing(
  225:                         reasons,
  226:                         MissingFact::GpuModel {
  227:                             gpu_id: gpu.gpu_id.clone(),
  228:                         },
  229:                     );
  230:                     false
  231:                 }
  232:             })
  233:             .collect::<Vec<_>>();
  234:         if model_unknown {
  235:             return;
  236:         }
  237:         if matched.len() < required_count as usize {
  238:             let mut allowed = job.allowed_gpu_models.clone();
  239:             allowed.sort();
  240:             allowed.dedup();
  241:             reasons.push(RejectionReason::GpuModelMismatch {
  242:                 matching: saturating_u32(matched.len()),
  243:                 required: required_count,
  244:                 allowed,
  245:             });
  246:             return;
  247:         }
  248:         matched
  249:     };
  250: 
  251:     let mut vram_unknown = false;
  252:     let matching_vram = model_matched
  253:         .iter()
  254:         .filter(|gpu| match gpu.available_vram_bytes {
  255:             Some(available) => available >= required_vram,
  256:             None => {
  257:                 vram_unknown = true;
  258:                 missing(
  259:                     reasons,
  260:                     MissingFact::GpuVram {
  261:                         gpu_id: gpu.gpu_id.clone(),
  262:                     },
  263:                 );
  264:                 false
  265:             }
  266:         })
  267:         .count();
  268:     if !vram_unknown && matching_vram < required_count as usize {
  269:         reasons.push(RejectionReason::GpuVramInsufficient {
  270:             matching: saturating_u32(matching_vram),
  271:             required: required_count,
  272:             minimum_bytes: required_vram,
  273:         });
  274:     }
  275: }

  277: fn evaluate_owner_policy(
  278:     candidate: &CandidateSnapshot,
  279:     job: &JobRequirements,
  280:     reasons: &mut Vec<RejectionReason>,
  281: ) {
  282:     let workload = match job.workload_class {
  283:         Some(value) => Some(value),
  284:         None => {
  285:             missing(reasons, MissingFact::JobWorkloadClass);
  286:             None
  287:         }
  288:     };
  289:     match (&candidate.allowed_workload_classes, workload) {
  290:         (None, _) => missing(reasons, MissingFact::AllowedWorkloadClasses),
  291:         (Some(allowed), Some(class)) if !allowed.contains(&class) => {
  292:             reasons.push(RejectionReason::WorkloadClassNotAllowed {
  293:                 workload_class: class,
  294:             });
  295:         }
  296:         _ => {}
  297:     }
  298: 
  299:     let owner = match candidate.owner_member_id.as_ref() {
  300:         Some(value) if !value.is_empty() => Some(value),
  301:         None | Some(_) => {
  302:             missing(reasons, MissingFact::OwnerMemberId);
  303:             None
  304:         }
  305:     };
  306:     let submitter = match job.submitter_member_id.as_ref() {
  307:         Some(value) if !value.is_empty() => Some(value),
  308:         None | Some(_) => {
  309:             missing(reasons, MissingFact::JobSubmitterMemberId);
  310:             None
  311:         }
  312:     };
  313:     if !matches!((owner, submitter), (Some(owner), Some(submitter)) if owner != submitter) {
  314:         return;
  315:     }
  316: 
  317:     if candidate.security_tier == Some(SecurityTier::S0) {
  318:         reasons.push(RejectionReason::ThirdPartyJobForbiddenOnS0);
  319:     }
  320:     if candidate.isolation_class != Some(IsolationClass::Restricted) {
  321:         return;
  322:     }
  323: 
  324:     match candidate.third_party_workloads_opt_in {
  325:         None => missing(reasons, MissingFact::ThirdPartyOptIn),
  326:         Some(false) => {
  327:             reasons.push(RejectionReason::ThirdPartyOptInRequiredOnRestrictedIsolation);
  328:         }
  329:         Some(true) => {}
  330:     }
  331:     match job.side_effect_class {
  332:         None => missing(reasons, MissingFact::JobSideEffectClass),
  333:         Some(SideEffectClass::Pure) => {}
  334:         Some(actual) => {
  335:             reasons.push(RejectionReason::ThirdPartyJobMustBePureOnRestrictedIsolation { actual })
  336:         }
  337:     }
  338:     match job.sensitivity {
  339:         None => missing(reasons, MissingFact::JobSensitivity),
  340:         Some(Sensitivity::Sensitive) => {
  341:             reasons.push(RejectionReason::ThirdPartySensitiveDataForbiddenOnRestrictedIsolation);
  342:         }
  343:         Some(Sensitivity::Public | Sensitivity::Internal) => {}
  344:     }
  345: }
  346: 
  347: fn compare_minimum<T: Copy + Ord>(
  348:     available: Option<T>,
  349:     required: Option<T>,
  350:     missing_available: MissingFact,
  351:     missing_required: MissingFact,
  352:     reasons: &mut Vec<RejectionReason>,
  353:     rejection: impl FnOnce(T, T) -> RejectionReason,
  354: ) {
  355:     if available.is_none() {
  356:         missing(reasons, missing_available);
  357:     }
  358:     if required.is_none() {
  359:         missing(reasons, missing_required);
  360:     }
  361:     if let (Some(available), Some(required)) = (available, required) {
  362:         if available < required {
  363:             reasons.push(rejection(available, required));
  364:         }
  365:     }
  366: }
  367: 
  368: fn compare_resource<T: Copy + Ord>(
  369:     available: Option<T>,
  370:     required: Option<T>,
  371:     missing_available: MissingFact,
  372:     missing_required: MissingFact,
  373:     reasons: &mut Vec<RejectionReason>,
  374:     rejection: impl FnOnce(T, T) -> RejectionReason,
  375: ) {
  376:     compare_minimum(
  377:         available,
  378:         required,
  379:         missing_available,
  380:         missing_required,
  381:         reasons,
  382:         rejection,
  383:     );
  384: }
  385: 
  386: fn missing(reasons: &mut Vec<RejectionReason>, fact: MissingFact) {
  387:     reasons.push(RejectionReason::MissingFact(fact));
  388: }
  389: 
  390: fn saturating_u32(value: usize) -> u32 {
  391:     u32::try_from(value).unwrap_or(u32::MAX)

   76: impl std::fmt::Display for ManifestConversionError {
   77:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
   78:         match self {

   82:             ),

   86:             ),

  102: impl std::error::Error for ManifestConversionError {}
  103: 
  104: /// ������ Manifest �� scheduler �Է����� �ű��.
  105: ///
  106: /// `submitter_member_id` �� **caller �� �ؼ��� ��**�̴� ? ��� ������
  107: /// "�� ���ڷ� �޴°�" �� ����.
  108: pub fn job_requirements_from_manifest(
  109:     manifest: &Verified<pb::JobManifest>,
  110:     submitter_member_id: &str,
  111: ) -> Result<JobRequirements, ManifestConversionError> {
  112:     // �� ���� �������� � �ʵ嵵 ���� �ʾҴ� ? `Verified::get()` ��
  113:     //   ������ �Ա���(`CLAUDE.md` ��0.2).
  114:     let m = manifest.get();
  115: 
  116:     if submitter_member_id.trim().is_empty() {
  117:         return Err(ManifestConversionError::MissingCallerFact(
  118:             "submitter_member_id",
  119:         ));
  120:     }
  121: 
  122:     let resources = m
  123:         .resources
  124:         .as_ref()
  125:         .ok_or(ManifestConversionError::MissingMessage("resources"))?;
  126:     let gpu = resources
  127:         .gpu
  128:         .as_ref()
  129:         .ok_or(ManifestConversionError::MissingMessage("resources.gpu"))?;
  130:     let workload = m
  131:         .workload
  132:         .as_ref()
  133:         .ok_or(ManifestConversionError::MissingMessage("workload"))?;
  134:     let dataset = m
  135:         .dataset
  136:         .as_ref()
  137:         .ok_or(ManifestConversionError::MissingMessage("dataset"))?;
  138: 
  139:     Ok(JobRequirements {
  140:         submitter_member_id: Some(submitter_member_id.to_string()),
  141:         workload_class: Some(workload_class(workload.class)?),
  142:         side_effect_class: Some(side_effect_class(m.side_effect_class)?),
  143:         sensitivity: Some(sensitivity(dataset.sensitivity)?),
  144:         minimum_security_tier: Some(security_tier(m.minimum_security_tier)?),
  145:         minimum_isolation_class: Some(isolation_class(m.minimum_isolation_class)?),
  146:         minimum_key_protection: Some(key_protection(m.minimum_key_protection)?),
  147:         // �� proto �� "�⺻ 1" �̶� �������Ƿ� 0 �� �̼����� �ƴ϶�
  148:         //   **����� �⺻��**�̴�. �̰� ����� ���� �ƴ϶� �Թ���
  149:         //   �״�� �ű�� ���̴�(`proto/common.proto` GpuRequest.min_count).
  150:         minimum_gpu_count: Some(if gpu.min_count == 0 { 1 } else { gpu.min_count }),
  151:         // 0 �� "VRAM ���� ����" �̴� ? �Թ��� ������ �䱸���� �ʴ´�.
  152:         minimum_vram_bytes_per_gpu: Some(gpu.min_vram_bytes),
  153:         // proto: "�� ��� = ���� ����".
  154:         allowed_gpu_models: gpu.allowed_gpu_models.clone(),
  155:         // �� 0 �� "0 ���� �䱸�Ѵ�" �� �ƴ϶� **�������� �ʾҴ�** �̴�.
  156:         //   proto �� �� �¿��� �⺻�� �ּ��� �� �޾Ҵ� ? ��
  157:         //   `UndeclaredAmount` �ּ� ����.
  158:         cpu_cores: Some(nonzero(resources.cpu_cores as u64, "resources.cpu_cores")? as u32),
  159:         ram_bytes: Some(nonzero(resources.ram_bytes, "resources.ram_bytes")?),
  160:         workspace_bytes: Some(nonzero(
  161:             resources.workspace_bytes,
  162:             "resources.workspace_bytes",
  163:         )?),
  164:     })
  165: }
  166: 
  167: /// 0 �̸� **�������� ���� ��**���� ���� �ź��Ѵ�.
  168: ///
  169: /// �� `min_vram_bytes` ���� ���� �ʴ´� ? ������ "���� ����" �̶��
  170: ///   �бⰡ ������ ���� �ְ�, 0 ���� �װ��� ǥ���� ����� ����.
  171: ///   �ٸ� �� �б⵵ �Թ��� �ٰŰ� ����(`proto/common.proto:188` ��
  172: ///   �ּ��� ����). �װ� �Թ� �����̶� ���⼭ ������ �ʴ´� ?
  173: ///   `docs/plans/_����_�۾�.md` �� �÷� �״�.
  174: fn nonzero(value: u64, field: &'static str) -> Result<u64, ManifestConversionError> {
  175:     if value == 0 {
  176:         return Err(ManifestConversionError::UndeclaredAmount(field));
  177:     }
  178:     Ok(value)
  179: }
  180: 
  181: // ---------------------------------------------------------------- enum ��
  182: //
  183: // �� �� �Լ��� **exhaustive `match`** ��. proto �� �� ������ ����
  184: //   `crates/protocol` �� �װ��� �˰� �Ǹ� **�������� ������** ? ������
  185: //   �� ǥ�� ������ ���� ���� ���´�(�� ����ҿ��� �� �� �޾���).
  186: 
  187: fn workload_class(value: i32) -> Result<WorkloadClass, ManifestConversionError> {
  188:     let parsed = pb::WorkloadClass::try_from(value).map_err(|_| {
  189:         ManifestConversionError::UnknownEnumValue {
  190:             field: "workload.class",
  191:             value,
  192:         }
  193:     })?;
  194:     match parsed {
  195:         pb::WorkloadClass::Unspecified => {
  196:             Err(ManifestConversionError::Unspecified("workload.class"))
  197:         }
  198:         pb::WorkloadClass::Training => Ok(WorkloadClass::Training),
  199:         pb::WorkloadClass::Inference => Ok(WorkloadClass::Inference),
  200:         pb::WorkloadClass::Preprocessing => Ok(WorkloadClass::Preprocessing),
  201:         pb::WorkloadClass::Evaluation => Ok(WorkloadClass::Evaluation),
  202:         pb::WorkloadClass::Rendering => Ok(WorkloadClass::Rendering),
  203:         pb::WorkloadClass::Other => Ok(WorkloadClass::Other),
  204:     }
  205: }
  206: 
  207: fn side_effect_class(value: i32) -> Result<SideEffectClass, ManifestConversionError> {
  208:     let parsed = pb::SideEffectClass::try_from(value).map_err(|_| {
  209:         ManifestConversionError::UnknownEnumValue {
  210:             field: "side_effect_class",
  211:             value,
  212:         }
  213:     })?;
  214:     match parsed {
  215:         pb::SideEffectClass::Unspecified => {
  216:             Err(ManifestConversionError::Unspecified("side_effect_class"))
  217:         }
  218:         pb::SideEffectClass::Pure => Ok(SideEffectClass::Pure),
  219:         pb::SideEffectClass::Idempotent => Ok(SideEffectClass::Idempotent),
  220:         pb::SideEffectClass::SideEffecting => Ok(SideEffectClass::SideEffecting),
  221:     }
  222: }
  223: 
  224: fn sensitivity(value: i32) -> Result<Sensitivity, ManifestConversionError> {
  225:     let parsed =
  226:         pb::Sensitivity::try_from(value).map_err(|_| ManifestConversionError::UnknownEnumValue {
  227:             field: "dataset.sensitivity",
  228:             value,
  229:         })?;
  230:     match parsed {
  231:         pb::Sensitivity::Unspecified => {
  232:             Err(ManifestConversionError::Unspecified("dataset.sensitivity"))
  233:         }
  234:         pb::Sensitivity::Public => Ok(Sensitivity::Public),
  235:         pb::Sensitivity::Internal => Ok(Sensitivity::Internal),
  236:         pb::Sensitivity::Sensitive => Ok(Sensitivity::Sensitive),
  237:     }
  238: }
  239: 
  240: fn security_tier(value: i32) -> Result<SecurityTier, ManifestConversionError> {

  243:             field: "minimum_security_tier",

  247:         pb::SecurityTier::Unspecified => Err(ManifestConversionError::Unspecified(
  248:             "minimum_security_tier",
  249:         )),
  250:         pb::SecurityTier::S0 => Ok(SecurityTier::S0),
  251:         pb::SecurityTier::S1 => Ok(SecurityTier::S1),
  252:         pb::SecurityTier::S2 => Ok(SecurityTier::S2),

  303:     /// ��� ���� ä���� Manifest. `tweak` ���� �� ������ ��� �ݷʸ� �����.

  353: 
  354:     #[test]
  355:     fn a_complete_manifest_becomes_complete_requirements() {
  356:         let requirements =
  357:             job_requirements_from_manifest(&verified(|_| {}), MEMBER).expect("��ȯ ����");
  358: 
  359:         // �� ��밪�� **������** ���´�. ��ȯ �Լ��� �ٽ� ���� �����
  360:         //   �� �Լ��� �ϰ��ǰ� Ʋ���� ����Ѵ�(�� ����ҿ��� �� �� ���Դ�).
  361:         assert_eq!(requirements.submitter_member_id.as_deref(), Some(MEMBER));

   55: fn evaluate(
   56:     snapshot: &GpuObservationSnapshot,
   57:     requirements: &JobGpuRequirements,
   58:     resources: &ScopeResourceInput,
   59: ) -> Result<ScopeCandidate, ScopeError> {
   60:     gpu_scope_candidate(snapshot, requirements, resources, ProvenanceGate::Verified)
   61: }
   62: 
   63: fn permutations<T: Clone>(items: &[T]) -> Vec<Vec<T>> {
   64:     fn visit<T: Clone>(remaining: Vec<T>, prefix: Vec<T>, output: &mut Vec<Vec<T>>) {
   65:         if remaining.is_empty() {
   66:             output.push(prefix);
   67:             return;
   68:         }
   69:         for index in 0..remaining.len() {
   70:             let mut next_remaining = remaining.clone();
   71:             let item = next_remaining.remove(index);
   72:             let mut next_prefix = prefix.clone();
   73:             next_prefix.push(item);
   74:             visit(next_remaining, next_prefix, output);
   75:         }
   76:     }
   77: 
   78:     let mut output = Vec::new();
   79:     visit(items.to_vec(), Vec::new(), &mut output);
   80:     output
   81: }

  147:         .allowed_compute_capabilities

  149:     let mut reverse_requirements = forward_requirements.clone();
  150:     reverse_requirements.allowed_gpu_models.reverse();
  151:     reverse_requirements.allowed_compute_capabilities.reverse();
  152:     let forward_resources = resources();
  153:     let mut reverse_resources = forward_resources.clone();
  154:     reverse_resources
  155:         .writable_prefixes
  156:         .as_mut()
  157:         .unwrap()
  158:         .reverse();
  159: 
  160:     assert_eq!(
  161:         evaluate(&input, &forward_requirements, &forward_resources),
  162:         evaluate(&input, &reverse_requirements, &reverse_resources)
  163:     );
  164: }

  228:     input.gpus.as_mut().unwrap()[0].healthy = None;

  240:                 gpu_id: "gpu-a".into(),

  281:             Err(ScopeError::MissingFact(expected))
  282:         );
  283:     }

  288:     unconstrained.allowed_gpu_models.clear();
  289:     let mut input = snapshot(vec![gpu("gpu-a", 10_000)]);

  372:         evaluate(
  373:             &snapshot(vec![gpu(" ", 10_000)]),

  375:             &resources(),
  376:         ),
  377:         Err(ScopeError::EmptyGpuId)
  378:     );
  379:     assert_eq!(
  380:         evaluate(
  381:             &snapshot(vec![gpu("gpu-a ", 10_000)]),

  383:             &resources(),

  385:         Err(ScopeError::NonCanonicalGpuId {
  386:             gpu_id: "gpu-a ".into()
  387:         })
  388:     );
  389: 
  390:     let duplicates = vec![gpu("gpu-a", 10_000), gpu("gpu-a", 20_000)];

  393:         &requirements(1, 1),
  394:         &resources(),
  395:     );
  396:     let reverse = evaluate(
  397:         &snapshot(duplicates.into_iter().rev().collect()),

  399:         &resources(),
  400:     );

  118: fn write_keyring(dir: &Path) -> PathBuf {
  119:     let keyring = dir.join("submitters.keyring");
  120:     let mut seed = [0u8; 32];
  121:     for (i, byte) in seed.iter_mut().enumerate() {
  122:         *byte = u8::from_str_radix(&SEED[i * 2..i * 2 + 2], 16).expect("seed hex");
  123:     }
  124:     let mut ring = gputeer_crypto::PersistentKeyring::new(
  125:         &keyring,
  126:         gputeer_crypto::KeyProtection::K0Plaintext,
  127:         gputeer_crypto::PlaintextPolicy::Allow,
  128:     )
  129:     .expect("keyring ����");
  130:     ring.insert_public(
  131:         SUBMITTER,
  132:         gputeer_crypto::SigningKey::from_bytes(&seed).verifying_key(),
  133:     )
  134:     .expect("����Ű ���");
  135:     ring.save().expect("keyring ����");
  136:     keyring
  137: }

  165: /// �� Job �� `submit` -> `import-manifest` -> `plan-job` ���� �ø���.
  166: fn queue_job(dir: &Path, keyring: &Path, db: &Path, job_id: &str, idem: &str) {
  167:     let manifest = dir.join(format!("{job_id}.pb"));
  168:     let issued = now_unix_ms().saturating_sub(60_000).to_string();
  169:     let expires = (now_unix_ms() + 7 * 24 * 3_600_000).to_string();
  170:     let mut args: Vec<&str> = vec![
  171:         "submit",
  172:         "--job-id",
  173:         job_id,
  174:         "--entrypoint",
  175:         "python",
  176:         "--submitter-device-id",
  177:         SUBMITTER,
  178:         "--submitter-seed",
  179:         SEED,
  180:         "--issued-at-unix-ms",
  181:         &issued,
  182:         "--expires-at-unix-ms",
  183:         &expires,
  184:         "--out",
  185:         manifest.to_str().unwrap(),
  186:     ];
  187:     args.extend_from_slice(&DECLARATIONS);
  188:     let (ok, output) = run_cli(&args);
  189:     assert!(ok, "submit ����: {output}");
  190: 
  191:     let (ok, output) = run_cli(&[
  192:         "import-manifest",
  193:         "--manifest",
  194:         manifest.to_str().unwrap(),
  195:         "--submitter-keyring",
  196:         keyring.to_str().unwrap(),
  197:         "--job-db",
  198:         db.to_str().unwrap(),
  199:         "--idempotency-key",
  200:         idem,
  201:         "--i-understand-plaintext-keyring-is-unsafe",
  202:         "true",
  203:     ]);
  204:     assert!(ok, "import-manifest ����: {output}");
  205: 
  206:     let (ok, output) = run_cli(&[
  207:         "plan-job",
  208:         "--job-id",
  209:         job_id,
  210:         "--control-db",
  211:         db.to_str().unwrap(),
  212:         "--submitter-keyring",
  213:         keyring.to_str().unwrap(),
  214:         "--submitter-member",
  215:         OWNER,
  216:         "--max-snapshot-age-ms",
  217:         "86400000",
  218:         "--i-understand-plaintext-keyring-is-unsafe",
  219:         "true",
  220:     ]);
  221:     assert!(ok, "plan-job ����: {output}");
  222: }

  224: #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
  225: fn stage(
  226:     keyring: &Path,
  227:     db: &Path,
  228:     job_id: &str,
  229:     attempt: &str,
  230:     lease: &str,
  231:     operation_key: &str,
  232: ) -> (bool, String) {
  233:     run_cli(&[
  234:         "stage-job",
  235:         "--job-id",
  236:         job_id,
  237:         "--control-db",
  238:         db.to_str().unwrap(),
  239:         "--submitter-keyring",
  240:         keyring.to_str().unwrap(),
  241:         "--submitter-member",
  242:         OWNER,
  243:         "--max-snapshot-age-ms",
  244:         "86400000",
  245:         "--best-fit-axes",
  246:         AXES,
  247:         "--coordinator-id",
  248:         COORDINATOR,
  249:         "--coordinator-term",
  250:         "1",
  251:         "--attempt-id",
  252:         attempt,
  253:         "--lease-id",
  254:         lease,
  255:         "--operation-key",
  256:         operation_key,
  257:         "--lease-issued-at-unix-ms",
  258:         LEASE_ISSUED,
  259:         "--lease-renew-after-unix-ms",
  260:         LEASE_RENEW,
  261:         "--lease-expires-at-unix-ms",
  262:         LEASE_EXPIRES,
  263:         "--lease-max-total-duration-seconds",
  264:         "86400",
  265:         "--i-understand-plaintext-keyring-is-unsafe",
  266:         "true",
  267:     ])
  268: }
  269: 
  270: fn job_state(db: &Path, job_id: &str) -> Option<JobState> {
  271:     let store = CoordinatorJobStore::open(db).expect("job store");
  272:     store.get(job_id).expect("��ȸ").map(|job| job.state)
  273: }

  275: /// �� Job �� �غ�� Ǯ�� �ø���.
  276: fn prepared(dir: &Path, job_id: &str) -> (PathBuf, PathBuf) {
  277:     let keyring = write_keyring(dir);
  278:     let db = dir.join("control.sqlite3");
  279:     let bootstrap = write_bootstrap(dir);
  280:     // inventory �� ���� �־�� plan-job �� �ĺ��� ����.
  281:     let (ok, output) = run_cli(&[
  282:         "import-inventory",
  283:         "--inventory",
  284:         bootstrap.to_str().unwrap(),
  285:         "--inventory-db",
  286:         db.to_str().unwrap(),
  287:     ]);
  288:     assert!(ok, "import-inventory ����: {output}");
  289:     queue_job(dir, &keyring, &db, job_id, "0102030405060708090a0b0c0d0e0f10");
  290:     (keyring, db)
  291: }

  293: /// ��尡 **��**�� Ǯ�� �����.
  294: ///
  295: /// �ڡ� 2026-09-10 ���� �˼� ���� ������ �����. ��尡 �ϳ���
  296: ///   "�̹� ����� ���" ������ �� ���� �ɷ���, **`QUEUED` ������
  297: ///   �����Ѵٴ� ������ �� ���� �� ����.** �� ������ ����� �� ��尡
  298: ///   ���� �־�� �Ѵ�.
  299: ///
  300: /// �� ���ڿ��� �߶� ������ �ʴ´� ? ó���� �׷��� �ߴٰ� JSON �� ������.
  301: ///   `agent_json()` ���� �� ���� ����� ��ģ��.
  302: fn prepared_two_nodes(dir: &Path, job_id: &str) -> (PathBuf, PathBuf) {
  303:     let keyring = write_keyring(dir);
  304:     let db = dir.join("control.sqlite3");
  305:     let bootstrap = dir.join("bootstrap_two.json");
  306:     let body = format!(
  307:         "{{
  308:   \"schema_version\": 1,
  309:   \"agents\": [
  310: {},
  311: {}
  312:   ]
  313: }}",
  314:         agent_json(NODE, GPU, "device-stage", 13),
  315:         // �� Ű�� �޶�� �Ѵ� ? inventory �� �ſ� �浹�� �ź��Ѵ�(DoD-44).
  316:         //   ó���� ���� Ű�� ��ٰ� BOOTSTRAP_REJECTED �� �޾Ҵ�.
  317:         agent_json("node-stage-b", "node-stage-b-gpu-0", "device-stage-b", 14),
  318:     );
  319:     std::fs::write(&bootstrap, body).expect("�� ��� ���� ����");
  320: 
  321:     let (ok, output) = run_cli(&[
  322:         "import-inventory",
  323:         "--inventory",
  324:         bootstrap.to_str().unwrap(),
  325:         "--inventory-db",
  326:         db.to_str().unwrap(),
  327:     ]);
  328:     assert!(ok, "import-inventory ����(��� ��): {output}");
  329:     queue_job(dir, &keyring, &db, job_id, "0102030405060708090a0b0c0d0e0f10");
  330:     (keyring, db)
  331: }

  333: /// agent �ϳ��� JSON. `write_bootstrap` �� ���� ���� ����.
  334: fn agent_json(node_id: &str, gpu_id: &str, device_id: &str, seed_byte: u8) -> String {
  335:     let key: String = gputeer_crypto::SigningKey::from_bytes(&[seed_byte; 32])
  336:         .verifying_key()
  337:         .to_bytes()
  338:         .iter()
  339:         .map(|b| format!("{b:02x}"))
  340:         .collect();
  341:     let observed = now_unix_ms();
  342:     format!(
  343:         r#"    {{
  344:       "registry": {{
  345:         "node_id": "{node_id}",
  346:         "device_id": "{device_id}",
  347:         "owner_member_id": "{OWNER}",
  348:         "verifying_key_hex": "{key}",
  349:         "node_state": "ONLINE",
  350:         "risk_state": "NORMAL",
  351:         "security_tier": "S2",
  352:         "isolation_class": "CONTAINED",
  353:         "key_protection": "K1"
  354:       }},
  355:       "inventory": {{
  356:         "inventory_revision": 1,
  357:         "observed_at_unix_ms": {observed},
  358:         "gpus": [
  359:           {{
  360:             "gpu_id": "{gpu_id}",
  361:             "model": "RTX 4070 SUPER",
  362:             "healthy": true,
  363:             "available_vram_bytes": 12884901888
  364:           }}
  365:         ],
  366:         "available_cpu_cores": 16,
  367:         "available_ram_bytes": 34359738368,
  368:         "available_workspace_bytes": 107374182400,
  369:         "allowed_workload_classes": ["TRAINING"],
  370:         "third_party_workloads_opt_in": true
  371:       }}
  372:     }}"#
  373:     )
  374: }

  495: fn staging_the_same_job_twice_is_idempotent() {

  521:             .expect("fence �� ��¿� ����")

  524:         fence_of(&first),

  527:     );

  726: #[test]
  727: fn a_non_durable_control_db_is_refused() {
  728:     let dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect("�ӽ� ���͸�");
  729:     let keyring = write_keyring(dir.path());
