    1: //! Single-node local atomic `QUEUED -> STAGING` storage kernel.
    2: //!
    3: //! One file, one SQLite connection, and one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction own
    4: //! the Job transition, Attempt/node creation, team-global fence allocation,
    5: //! Lease insertion, and operation idempotency record. The reservation-aware
    6: //! entrypoint additionally compares the selected Agent inventory revision and
    7: //! validates and binds the selected GPU IDs to a node-exclusive reservation in
    8: //! that same transaction. This is local durable state only; it is not a
    9: //! Raft/ControlStore `COMMITTED` transition and does not dispatch a Grant.
   10: 
   11: use std::path::Path;
   12: 
   13: use rusqlite::{Connection, Error as SqlError, ErrorCode, OptionalExtension, TransactionBehavior};
   14: 
   15: use crate::job_store::{self, JobState, StoredJob};
   16: use crate::lease_store::{self, StoredLease};
   17: 
   18: const BUSY_TIMEOUT: std::time::Duration = std::time::Duration::from_secs(1);
   19: 
   20: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
   21: pub struct StageQueuedRequest {
   22:     pub operation_key: [u8; 16],
   23:     pub job_id: String,
   24:     pub attempt_id: String,
   25:     pub lease_id: String,
   26:     pub node_id: String,
   27:     /// Canonical scheduler GPU IDs selected from the admitted inventory revision.
   28:     ///
   29:     /// The reservation-aware entrypoint requires a non-empty, strictly sorted
   30:     /// list. The legacy reservation-free entrypoint deliberately ignores it.
   31:     pub selected_gpu_ids: Vec<String>,
   32:     pub issuing_coordinator_id: String,
   33:     pub coordinator_term: u64,
   34:     pub issued_at_unix_ms: u64,
   35:     pub renew_after_unix_ms: u64,
   36:     pub expires_at_unix_ms: u64,
   37:     pub max_total_duration_seconds: u64,
   38: }
   39: 
   40: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
   41: pub enum AttemptState {
   42:     Created,
   43: }
   44: 
   45: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
   46: pub struct StoredAttempt {
   47:     pub attempt_id: String,
   48:     pub job_id: String,
   49:     pub state: AttemptState,
   50:     pub node_ids: Vec<String>,
   51:     pub fence_epoch: u64,
   52:     pub lease_id: String,
   53:     pub created_at_unix_ms: u64,
   54:     pub revision: u64,
   55: }
   56: 
   57: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
   58: pub struct StageQueuedResult {
   59:     pub job: StoredJob,
   60:     pub attempt: StoredAttempt,
   61:     pub lease: StoredLease,
   62:     /// `false` means the operation key replayed its original durable result.
   63:     pub created: bool,
   64: }
   65: 
   66: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
   67: pub struct StoredNodeReservation {
   68:     pub node_id: String,
   69:     pub job_id: String,
   70:     pub attempt_id: String,
   71:     pub inventory_revision: u64,
   72:     pub reserved_at_unix_ms: u64,
   73:     pub selected_gpu_ids: Vec<String>,
   74: }
   75: 
   76: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
   77: pub struct ReservedStageResult {
   78:     pub reservation: StoredNodeReservation,
   79:     pub stage: StageQueuedResult,
   80: }
   81: 
   82: #[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
   83: pub enum StagingStoreError {
   84:     InvalidInput(&'static str),
   85:     JobNotFound,
   86:     JobNotQueued(JobState),
   87:     OperationConflict,
   88:     AttemptIdConflict(String),
   89:     LeaseIdConflict(String),
   90:     ClockRollback {
   91:         issued_at_unix_ms: u64,
   92:         queued_at_unix_ms: u64,
   93:     },
   94:     InvalidLeaseLifetime,
   95:     FenceEpochOverflow,
   96:     CorruptData(String),
   97:     Io(String),
   98:     LockTimeout,
   99:     #[cfg(test)]
  100:     InjectedFailure(&'static str),
  101: }
  102: 
  103: impl std::fmt::Display for StagingStoreError {
  104:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
  105:         match self {
  106:             Self::InvalidInput(field) => write!(f, "invalid staging input: {field}"),
  107:             Self::JobNotFound => write!(f, "job_id is not present in the control DB"),
  108:             Self::JobNotQueued(state) => write!(f, "Job is not QUEUED: {state:?}"),
  109:             Self::OperationConflict => write!(f, "staging operation key payload conflict"),
  110:             Self::AttemptIdConflict(id) => write!(f, "attempt_id already exists: {id}"),
  111:             Self::LeaseIdConflict(id) => write!(f, "lease_id already exists: {id}"),
  112:             Self::ClockRollback { issued_at_unix_ms, queued_at_unix_ms } => write!(
  113:                 f,
  114:                 "staging clock moved backwards: issued={issued_at_unix_ms}, queued={queued_at_unix_ms}"
  115:             ),
  116:             Self::InvalidLeaseLifetime => write!(f, "invalid Lease lifetime ordering"),
  117:             Self::FenceEpochOverflow => write!(f, "team-global fence epoch overflow"),
  118:             Self::CorruptData(message) => write!(f, "staging store corruption: {message}"),
  119:             Self::Io(message) => write!(f, "staging store I/O error: {message}"),
  120:             Self::LockTimeout => write!(f, "staging store lock acquisition timed out"),
  121:             #[cfg(test)]
  122:             Self::InjectedFailure(point) => write!(f, "injected staging failure: {point}"),
  123:         }
  124:     }
  125: }
  126: 
  127: impl std::error::Error for StagingStoreError {}
  128: 
  129: #[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
  130: pub enum ReservedStageError {
  131:     InventoryMissing {
  132:         node_id: String,
  133:     },
  134:     InventoryRevisionMismatch {
  135:         node_id: String,
  136:         expected: u64,
  137:         actual: u64,
  138:     },
  139:     SelectedGpuMissing {
  140:         node_id: String,
  141:         gpu_id: String,
  142:     },
  143:     NodeAlreadyReserved {
  144:         node_id: String,
  145:         owning_job_id: String,
  146:         owning_attempt_id: String,
  147:     },
  148:     Staging(StagingStoreError),
  149: }
  150: 
  151: impl std::fmt::Display for ReservedStageError {
  152:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
  153:         match self {
  154:             Self::InventoryMissing { node_id } => {
  155:                 write!(f, "inventory is missing for selected node: {node_id}")
  156:             }
  157:             Self::InventoryRevisionMismatch {
  158:                 node_id,
  159:                 expected,
  160:                 actual,
  161:             } => write!(
  162:                 f,
  163:                 "inventory revision CAS conflict for {node_id}: expected={expected}, actual={actual}"
  164:             ),
  165:             Self::SelectedGpuMissing { node_id, gpu_id } => write!(
  166:                 f,
  167:                 "selected GPU is missing from admitted inventory: node={node_id}, gpu={gpu_id}"
  168:             ),
  169:             Self::NodeAlreadyReserved {
  170:                 node_id,
  171:                 owning_job_id,
  172:                 owning_attempt_id,
  173:             } => write!(
  174:                 f,
  175:                 "node is already reserved: node={node_id}, job={owning_job_id}, attempt={owning_attempt_id}"
  176:             ),
  177:             Self::Staging(error) => write!(f, "durable staging failed: {error}"),
  178:         }
  179:     }
  180: }
  181: 
  182: impl std::error::Error for ReservedStageError {
  183:     fn source(&self) -> Option<&(dyn std::error::Error + 'static)> {
  184:         match self {
  185:             Self::Staging(error) => Some(error),
  186:             _ => None,
  187:         }
  188:     }
  189: }
  190: 
  191: impl From<StagingStoreError> for ReservedStageError {
  192:     fn from(error: StagingStoreError) -> Self {
  193:         Self::Staging(error)
  194:     }
  195: }
  196: 
  197: pub struct CoordinatorStagingStore {
  198:     connection: Connection,
  199: }
  200: 
  201: pub(crate) fn initialize_schema(connection: &mut Connection) -> Result<(), StagingStoreError> {
  202:     job_store::initialize_schema(connection).map_err(map_job_error)?;
  203:     lease_store::initialize_schema(connection).map_err(map_lease_error)?;
  204:     connection
  205:         .execute_batch(
  206:             r#"
  207:             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS coordinator_attempts (
  208:                 attempt_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  209:                 job_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES coordinator_jobs(job_id),
  210:                 state TEXT NOT NULL,
  211:                 fence_epoch BLOB NOT NULL,
  212:                 lease_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  213:                 created_at_unix_ms BLOB NOT NULL,
  214:                 revision BLOB NOT NULL
  215:             );
  216:             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS coordinator_attempt_nodes (
  217:                 attempt_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES coordinator_attempts(attempt_id),
  218:                 node_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  219:                 ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL,
  220:                 PRIMARY KEY(attempt_id, ordinal),
  221:                 UNIQUE(attempt_id, node_id)
  222:             );
  223:             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS coordinator_fence_state (
  224:                 singleton INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(singleton = 1),
  225:                 max_issued_epoch BLOB NOT NULL
  226:             );
  227:             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS staging_operation_idempotency (
  228:                 operation_key BLOB PRIMARY KEY CHECK(length(operation_key) = 16),
  229:                 job_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES coordinator_jobs(job_id),
  230:                 attempt_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES coordinator_attempts(attempt_id),
  231:                 lease_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES coordinator_leases(lease_id),
  232:                 request_payload BLOB NOT NULL,
  233:                 fence_epoch BLOB NOT NULL
  234:             );
  235:             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS coordinator_node_reservations (
  236:                 node_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY
  237:                     REFERENCES coordinator_agent_inventory(node_id),
  238:                 job_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES coordinator_jobs(job_id),
  239:                 attempt_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE
  240:                     REFERENCES coordinator_attempts(attempt_id)
  241:                     DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED,
  242:                 inventory_revision BLOB NOT NULL,
  243:                 reserved_at_unix_ms BLOB NOT NULL
  244:             );
  245:             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS coordinator_node_reservation_gpus (
  246:                 node_id TEXT NOT NULL
  247:                     REFERENCES coordinator_node_reservations(node_id),
  248:                 gpu_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  249:                 ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(ordinal >= 0),
  250:                 PRIMARY KEY(node_id, ordinal),
  251:                 UNIQUE(node_id, gpu_id)
  252:             );
  253:             "#,
  254:         )
  255:         .map_err(map_sql_error)
  256: }
  257: 
  258: impl CoordinatorStagingStore {
  259:     pub fn open(path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> Result<Self, StagingStoreError> {
  260:         let mut connection = Connection::open(path).map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  261:         connection
  262:             .busy_timeout(BUSY_TIMEOUT)
  263:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  264:         initialize_schema(&mut connection)?;
  265:         Ok(Self { connection })
  266:     }
  267: 
  268:     pub fn is_durable(&self) -> bool {
  269:         matches!(self.connection.path(), Some(path) if !path.is_empty() && path != ":memory:")
  270:     }
  271: 
  272:     pub fn get_attempt(
  273:         &self,
  274:         attempt_id: &str,
  275:     ) -> Result<Option<StoredAttempt>, StagingStoreError> {
  276:         fetch_attempt(&self.connection, attempt_id)
  277:     }
  278: 
  279:     pub fn fence_epoch(&self) -> Result<Option<u64>, StagingStoreError> {
  280:         read_counter(&self.connection)
  281:     }
  282: 
  283:     pub fn get_node_reservation(
  284:         &self,
  285:         node_id: &str,
  286:     ) -> Result<Option<StoredNodeReservation>, StagingStoreError> {
  287:         fetch_node_reservation(&self.connection, node_id)
  288:     }
  289: 
  290:     pub fn stage_queued_with_lease(
  291:         &mut self,
  292:         request: &StageQueuedRequest,
  293:     ) -> Result<StageQueuedResult, StagingStoreError> {
  294:         self.stage(request, None)
  295:     }
  296: 
  297:     pub fn reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(
  298:         &mut self,
  299:         request: &StageQueuedRequest,
  300:         expected_inventory_revision: u64,
  301:     ) -> Result<ReservedStageResult, ReservedStageError> {
  302:         self.reserve_and_stage(request, expected_inventory_revision, None)
  303:     }
  304: 
  305:     fn stage(
  306:         &mut self,
  307:         request: &StageQueuedRequest,
  308:         fault: Option<TestFault>,
  309:     ) -> Result<StageQueuedResult, StagingStoreError> {
  310:         validate_request(request)?;
  311:         let request_payload = encode_request(request);
  312:         let transaction = self
  313:             .connection
  314:             .transaction_with_behavior(TransactionBehavior::Immediate)
  315:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  316: 
  317:         if let Some(operation) = fetch_operation(&transaction, &request.operation_key)? {
  318:             if operation.job_id != request.job_id
  319:                 || operation.attempt_id != request.attempt_id
  320:                 || operation.lease_id != request.lease_id
  321:                 || operation.request_payload != request_payload
  322:             {
  323:                 return Err(StagingStoreError::OperationConflict);
  324:             }
  325:             let result = load_result(&transaction, request, operation.fence_epoch)?;
  326:             transaction.commit().map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  327:             return Ok(StageQueuedResult {
  328:                 created: false,
  329:                 ..result
  330:             });
  331:         }
  332: 
  333:         let result = stage_new_in_transaction(&transaction, request, &request_payload, fault)?;
  334:         transaction.commit().map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  335:         Ok(result)
  336:     }
  337: 
  338:     fn reserve_and_stage(
  339:         &mut self,
  340:         request: &StageQueuedRequest,
  341:         expected_inventory_revision: u64,
  342:         fault: Option<TestFault>,
  343:     ) -> Result<ReservedStageResult, ReservedStageError> {
  344:         validate_request(request)?;
  345:         validate_selected_gpu_ids(&request.selected_gpu_ids)?;
  346:         let request_payload = encode_reserved_request(request, expected_inventory_revision);
  347:         let transaction = self
  348:             .connection
  349:             .transaction_with_behavior(TransactionBehavior::Immediate)
  350:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  351: 
  352:         if let Some(operation) = fetch_operation(&transaction, &request.operation_key)? {
  353:             if operation.job_id != request.job_id
  354:                 || operation.attempt_id != request.attempt_id
  355:                 || operation.lease_id != request.lease_id
  356:                 || operation.request_payload != request_payload
  357:             {
  358:                 return Err(StagingStoreError::OperationConflict.into());
  359:             }
  360:             let stage = load_result(&transaction, request, operation.fence_epoch)?;
  361:             let reservation =
  362:                 fetch_node_reservation(&transaction, &request.node_id)?.ok_or_else(|| {
  363:                     StagingStoreError::CorruptData(
  364:                         "reserved staging operation points to missing reservation".into(),
  365:                     )
  366:                 })?;
  367:             validate_replayed_reservation(&reservation, request, expected_inventory_revision)?;
  368:             transaction.commit().map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  369:             return Ok(ReservedStageResult {
  370:                 reservation,
  371:                 stage: StageQueuedResult {
  372:                     created: false,
  373:                     ..stage
  374:                 },
  375:             });
  376:         }
  377: 
  378:         let actual_inventory_revision = fetch_inventory_revision(&transaction, &request.node_id)?
  379:             .ok_or_else(|| ReservedStageError::InventoryMissing {
  380:             node_id: request.node_id.clone(),
  381:         })?;
  382:         if actual_inventory_revision != expected_inventory_revision {
  383:             return Err(ReservedStageError::InventoryRevisionMismatch {
  384:                 node_id: request.node_id.clone(),
  385:                 expected: expected_inventory_revision,
  386:                 actual: actual_inventory_revision,
  387:             });
  388:         }
  389:         validate_selected_gpus_exist(&transaction, &request.node_id, &request.selected_gpu_ids)?;
  390:         if let Some(existing) = fetch_node_reservation(&transaction, &request.node_id)? {
  391:             return Err(ReservedStageError::NodeAlreadyReserved {
  392:                 node_id: existing.node_id,
  393:                 owning_job_id: existing.job_id,
  394:                 owning_attempt_id: existing.attempt_id,
  395:             });
  396:         }
  397: 
  398:         let reservation = StoredNodeReservation {
  399:             node_id: request.node_id.clone(),
  400:             job_id: request.job_id.clone(),
  401:             attempt_id: request.attempt_id.clone(),
  402:             inventory_revision: expected_inventory_revision,
  403:             reserved_at_unix_ms: request.issued_at_unix_ms,
  404:             selected_gpu_ids: request.selected_gpu_ids.clone(),
  405:         };
  406:         insert_node_reservation(&transaction, &reservation)?;
  407:         fail_at(fault, TestFault::AfterReservationInsert)?;
  408:         insert_gpu_binding(&transaction, &reservation)?;
  409:         fail_at(fault, TestFault::AfterGpuBindingInsert)?;
  410:         let stage = stage_new_in_transaction(&transaction, request, &request_payload, fault)?;
  411:         transaction.commit().map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  412:         Ok(ReservedStageResult { reservation, stage })
  413:     }
  414: }
  415: 
  416: fn stage_new_in_transaction(
  417:     connection: &Connection,
  418:     request: &StageQueuedRequest,
  419:     request_payload: &[u8],
  420:     fault: Option<TestFault>,
  421: ) -> Result<StageQueuedResult, StagingStoreError> {
  422:     let mut job = job_store::fetch_job(connection, &request.job_id)
  423:         .map_err(map_job_error)?
  424:         .ok_or(StagingStoreError::JobNotFound)?;
  425:     if job.state != JobState::Queued {
  426:         return Err(StagingStoreError::JobNotQueued(job.state));
  427:     }
  428:     let queued_at = job.queued_at_unix_ms.ok_or_else(|| {
  429:         StagingStoreError::CorruptData("QUEUED Job has no queued timestamp".into())
  430:     })?;
  431:     if request.issued_at_unix_ms < queued_at {
  432:         return Err(StagingStoreError::ClockRollback {
  433:             issued_at_unix_ms: request.issued_at_unix_ms,
  434:             queued_at_unix_ms: queued_at,
  435:         });
  436:     }
  437:     if fetch_attempt(connection, &request.attempt_id)?.is_some() {
  438:         return Err(StagingStoreError::AttemptIdConflict(
  439:             request.attempt_id.clone(),
  440:         ));
  441:     }
  442:     if lease_store::fetch_lease(connection, &request.lease_id)
  443:         .map_err(map_lease_error)?
  444:         .is_some()
  445:     {
  446:         return Err(StagingStoreError::LeaseIdConflict(request.lease_id.clone()));
  447:     }
  448: 
  449:     let epoch = allocate_epoch(connection)?;
  450:     let attempt = StoredAttempt {
  451:         attempt_id: request.attempt_id.clone(),
  452:         job_id: request.job_id.clone(),
  453:         state: AttemptState::Created,
  454:         node_ids: vec![request.node_id.clone()],
  455:         fence_epoch: epoch,
  456:         lease_id: request.lease_id.clone(),
  457:         created_at_unix_ms: request.issued_at_unix_ms,
  458:         revision: 0,
  459:     };
  460:     insert_attempt(connection, &attempt)?;
  461:     fail_at(fault, TestFault::AfterAttemptInsert)?;
  462: 
  463:     let lease = request.to_lease(epoch);
  464:     lease_store::insert_lease(connection, &lease).map_err(map_lease_error)?;
  465:     fail_at(fault, TestFault::AfterLeaseInsert)?;
  466:     fail_at(fault, TestFault::BeforeJobUpdate)?;
  467: 
  468:     job.state = JobState::Staging;
  469:     job.staging_at_unix_ms = Some(request.issued_at_unix_ms);
  470:     job.revision = job
  471:         .revision
  472:         .checked_add(1)
  473:         .ok_or_else(|| StagingStoreError::CorruptData("job revision overflow".into()))?;
  474:     job_store::update_job(connection, &job).map_err(map_job_error)?;
  475:     insert_operation(connection, request, request_payload, epoch)?;
  476:     Ok(StageQueuedResult {
  477:         job,
  478:         attempt,
  479:         lease,
  480:         created: true,
  481:     })
  482: }
  483: 
  484: impl StageQueuedRequest {
  485:     fn to_lease(&self, fence_epoch: u64) -> StoredLease {
  486:         StoredLease {
  487:             lease_id: self.lease_id.clone(),
  488:             job_id: self.job_id.clone(),
  489:             attempt_id: self.attempt_id.clone(),
  490:             holder_node_id: self.node_id.clone(),
  491:             fence_epoch,
  492:             expires_at_unix_ms: self.expires_at_unix_ms,
  493:             issuing_coordinator_id: self.issuing_coordinator_id.clone(),
  494:             coordinator_term: self.coordinator_term,
  495:             issued_at_unix_ms: self.issued_at_unix_ms,
  496:             renew_after_unix_ms: self.renew_after_unix_ms,
  497:             max_total_duration_seconds: self.max_total_duration_seconds,
  498:             revoked_at_unix_ms: None,
  499:         }
  500:     }
  501: }
  502: 
  503: fn validate_request(request: &StageQueuedRequest) -> Result<(), StagingStoreError> {
  504:     for (name, value) in [
  505:         ("job_id", &request.job_id),
  506:         ("attempt_id", &request.attempt_id),
  507:         ("lease_id", &request.lease_id),
  508:         ("node_id", &request.node_id),
  509:         ("issuing_coordinator_id", &request.issuing_coordinator_id),  522:         .ok_or(StagingStoreError::InvalidLeaseLifetime)?;  525:         || request.renew_after_unix_ms >= request.expires_at_unix_ms
  526:         || lifetime_ms > max_duration_ms
  527:     {
  528:         return Err(StagingStoreError::InvalidLeaseLifetime);
  529:     }
  530:     Ok(())
  531: }
  532: 
  533: fn validate_selected_gpu_ids(selected_gpu_ids: &[String]) -> Result<(), StagingStoreError> {
  534:     if selected_gpu_ids.is_empty() {
  535:         return Err(StagingStoreError::InvalidInput("selected_gpu_ids"));
  536:     }
  537:     for gpu_id in selected_gpu_ids {
  538:         if gpu_id.trim().is_empty() {
  539:             return Err(StagingStoreError::InvalidInput(
  540:                 "selected_gpu_ids blank gpu_id",
  541:             ));
  542:         }
  543:     }
  544:     for pair in selected_gpu_ids.windows(2) {
  545:         if pair[0] == pair[1] {
  546:             return Err(StagingStoreError::InvalidInput(
  547:                 "selected_gpu_ids duplicate gpu_id",
  548:             ));
  549:         }
  550:         if pair[0] > pair[1] {
  551:             return Err(StagingStoreError::InvalidInput(
  552:                 "selected_gpu_ids canonical order",
  553:             ));
  554:         }
  555:     }
  556:     Ok(())
  557: }
  558: 
  559: fn allocate_epoch(connection: &Connection) -> Result<u64, StagingStoreError> {
  560:     let mut base = read_counter(connection)?.unwrap_or(0);
  561:     let mut statement = connection
  562:         .prepare("SELECT fence_epoch FROM coordinator_leases")
  563:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  564:     let rows = statement
  565:         .query_map([], |row| row.get::<_, Vec<u8>>(0))
  566:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  567:     for row in rows {
  568:         base = base.max(decode_u64(
  569:             &row.map_err(map_sql_error)?,
  570:             "lease fence_epoch",
  571:         )?);
  572:     }
  573:     let epoch = base
  574:         .checked_add(1)
  575:         .ok_or(StagingStoreError::FenceEpochOverflow)?;
  576:     connection
  577:         .execute(
  578:             "INSERT INTO coordinator_fence_state(singleton, max_issued_epoch) VALUES (1, ?1)
  579:              ON CONFLICT(singleton) DO UPDATE SET max_issued_epoch = excluded.max_issued_epoch",
  580:             rusqlite::params![encode_u64(epoch)],
  581:         )
  582:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  583:     Ok(epoch)
  584: }
  585: 
  586: fn read_counter(connection: &Connection) -> Result<Option<u64>, StagingStoreError> {
  587:     connection
  588:         .query_row(
  589:             "SELECT max_issued_epoch FROM coordinator_fence_state WHERE singleton = 1",
  590:             [],
  591:             |row| row.get::<_, Vec<u8>>(0),
  592:         )
  593:         .optional()
  594:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?
  595:         .map(|bytes| decode_u64(&bytes, "max_issued_epoch"))
  596:         .transpose()
  597: }
  598: 
  599: fn insert_attempt(
  600:     connection: &Connection,
  601:     attempt: &StoredAttempt,
  602: ) -> Result<(), StagingStoreError> {
  603:     connection
  604:         .execute(
  605:             "INSERT INTO coordinator_attempts(
  606:                 attempt_id, job_id, state, fence_epoch, lease_id, created_at_unix_ms, revision
  607:              ) VALUES (?1, ?2, 'CREATED', ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6)",
  608:             rusqlite::params![
  609:                 attempt.attempt_id,
  610:                 attempt.job_id,
  611:                 encode_u64(attempt.fence_epoch),
  612:                 attempt.lease_id,
  613:                 encode_u64(attempt.created_at_unix_ms),
  614:                 encode_u64(attempt.revision),
  615:             ],
  616:         )
  617:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  618:     connection
  619:         .execute(
  620:             "INSERT INTO coordinator_attempt_nodes(attempt_id, node_id, ordinal) VALUES (?1, ?2, 0)",
  621:             rusqlite::params![attempt.attempt_id, attempt.node_ids[0]],
  622:         )
  623:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  624:     Ok(())
  625: }
  626: 
  627: pub(crate) fn fetch_attempt(
  628:     connection: &Connection,
  629:     attempt_id: &str,
  630: ) -> Result<Option<StoredAttempt>, StagingStoreError> {
  631:     let raw = connection
  632:         .query_row(
  633:             "SELECT attempt_id, job_id, state, fence_epoch, lease_id, created_at_unix_ms, revision
  634:              FROM coordinator_attempts WHERE attempt_id = ?1",
  635:             rusqlite::params![attempt_id],
  636:             |row| {
  637:                 Ok((
  638:                     row.get::<_, String>(0)?,
  639:                     row.get::<_, String>(1)?,
  640:                     row.get::<_, String>(2)?,
  641:                     row.get::<_, Vec<u8>>(3)?,
  642:                     row.get::<_, String>(4)?,
  643:                     row.get::<_, Vec<u8>>(5)?,
  644:                     row.get::<_, Vec<u8>>(6)?,
  645:                 ))
  646:             },
  647:         )
  648:         .optional()
  649:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  650:     let Some((attempt_id, job_id, state, epoch, lease_id, created_at, revision)) = raw else {
  651:         return Ok(None);
  652:     };
  653:     if state != "CREATED" {
  654:         return Err(StagingStoreError::CorruptData(format!(
  655:             "unknown Attempt state: {state}"
  656:         )));
  657:     }
  658:     let mut statement = connection
  659:         .prepare("SELECT node_id, ordinal FROM coordinator_attempt_nodes WHERE attempt_id = ?1 ORDER BY ordinal")
  660:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  661:     let nodes = statement
  662:         .query_map(rusqlite::params![attempt_id], |row| {
  663:             Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, i64>(1)?))
  664:         })
  665:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?
  666:         .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
  667:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  668:     if nodes.len() != 1 || nodes[0].1 != 0 || nodes[0].0.trim().is_empty() {
  669:         return Err(StagingStoreError::CorruptData(
  670:             "single-node Attempt row shape is invalid".into(),
  671:         ));
  672:     }
  673:     Ok(Some(StoredAttempt {
  674:         attempt_id,
  675:         job_id,
  676:         state: AttemptState::Created,
  677:         node_ids: vec![nodes[0].0.clone()],
  678:         fence_epoch: decode_u64(&epoch, "Attempt fence_epoch")?,
  679:         lease_id,
  680:         created_at_unix_ms: decode_u64(&created_at, "Attempt created_at_unix_ms")?,
  681:         revision: decode_u64(&revision, "Attempt revision")?,
  682:     }))
  683: }
  684: 
  685: #[derive(Debug)]
  686: struct StoredOperation {
  687:     job_id: String,
  688:     attempt_id: String,
  689:     lease_id: String,
  690:     request_payload: Vec<u8>,
  691:     fence_epoch: u64,
  692: }
  693: 
  694: fn fetch_operation(
  695:     connection: &Connection,
  696:     key: &[u8; 16],
  697: ) -> Result<Option<StoredOperation>, StagingStoreError> {
  698:     connection
  699:         .query_row(
  700:             "SELECT job_id, attempt_id, lease_id, request_payload, fence_epoch
  701:              FROM staging_operation_idempotency WHERE operation_key = ?1",
  702:             rusqlite::params![key.as_slice()],
  703:             |row| {
  704:                 Ok((
  705:                     row.get::<_, String>(0)?,
  706:                     row.get::<_, String>(1)?,
  707:                     row.get::<_, String>(2)?,
  708:                     row.get::<_, Vec<u8>>(3)?,
  709:                     row.get::<_, Vec<u8>>(4)?,
  710:                 ))
  711:             },
  712:         )
  713:         .optional()
  714:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?
  715:         .map(|(job_id, attempt_id, lease_id, request_payload, epoch)| {
  716:             Ok(StoredOperation {
  717:                 job_id,
  718:                 attempt_id,
  719:                 lease_id,
  720:                 request_payload,
  721:                 fence_epoch: decode_u64(&epoch, "operation fence_epoch")?,
  722:             })
  723:         })
  724:         .transpose()
  725: }
  726: 
  727: fn insert_operation(
  728:     connection: &Connection,
  729:     request: &StageQueuedRequest,
  730:     request_payload: &[u8],
  731:     epoch: u64,
  732: ) -> Result<(), StagingStoreError> {
  733:     connection
  734:         .execute(
  735:             "INSERT INTO staging_operation_idempotency(
  736:             operation_key, job_id, attempt_id, lease_id, request_payload, fence_epoch
  737:          ) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6)",
  738:             rusqlite::params![
  739:                 request.operation_key.as_slice(),
  740:                 request.job_id,
  741:                 request.attempt_id,
  742:                 request.lease_id,
  743:                 request_payload,
  744:                 encode_u64(epoch)
  745:             ],
  746:         )
  747:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  748:     Ok(())
  749: }
  750: 
  751: fn encode_request(request: &StageQueuedRequest) -> Vec<u8> {
  752:     let mut payload = Vec::new();
  753:     for value in [
  754:         &request.job_id,
  755:         &request.attempt_id,
  756:         &request.lease_id,
  757:         &request.node_id,
  758:         &request.issuing_coordinator_id,
  759:     ] {
  760:         payload.extend_from_slice(&(value.len() as u64).to_be_bytes());
  761:         payload.extend_from_slice(value.as_bytes());
  762:     }
  763:     for value in [
  764:         request.coordinator_term,
  765:         request.issued_at_unix_ms,
  766:         request.renew_after_unix_ms,
  767:         request.expires_at_unix_ms,
  768:         request.max_total_duration_seconds,
  769:     ] {
  770:         payload.extend_from_slice(&value.to_be_bytes());
  771:     }
  772:     payload
  773: }
  774: 
  775: fn encode_reserved_request(
  776:     request: &StageQueuedRequest,
  777:     expected_inventory_revision: u64,
  778: ) -> Vec<u8> {
  779:     let mut payload = encode_request(request);
  780:     payload.extend_from_slice(&expected_inventory_revision.to_be_bytes());
  781:     payload.extend_from_slice(&(request.selected_gpu_ids.len() as u64).to_be_bytes());
  782:     for gpu_id in &request.selected_gpu_ids {
  783:         payload.extend_from_slice(&(gpu_id.len() as u64).to_be_bytes());
  784:         payload.extend_from_slice(gpu_id.as_bytes());
  785:     }
  786:     payload
  787: }
  788: 
  789: fn fetch_inventory_revision(
  790:     connection: &Connection,
  791:     node_id: &str,
  792: ) -> Result<Option<u64>, StagingStoreError> {
  793:     connection
  794:         .query_row(
  795:             "SELECT inventory_revision FROM coordinator_agent_inventory WHERE node_id = ?1",
  796:             rusqlite::params![node_id],
  797:             |row| row.get::<_, Vec<u8>>(0),
  798:         )
  799:         .optional()
  800:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?
  801:         .map(|bytes| decode_u64(&bytes, "inventory_revision"))
  802:         .transpose()
  803: }
  804: 
  805: fn validate_selected_gpus_exist(
  806:     connection: &Connection,
  807:     node_id: &str,
  808:     selected_gpu_ids: &[String],
  809: ) -> Result<(), ReservedStageError> {
  810:     for gpu_id in selected_gpu_ids {
  811:         let exists = connection
  812:             .query_row(
  813:                 "SELECT 1 FROM coordinator_agent_gpus WHERE node_id = ?1 AND gpu_id = ?2",
  814:                 rusqlite::params![node_id, gpu_id],
  815:                 |_| Ok(()),
  816:             )
  817:             .optional()
  818:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?
  819:             .is_some();
  820:         if !exists {
  821:             return Err(ReservedStageError::SelectedGpuMissing {
  822:                 node_id: node_id.to_owned(),
  823:                 gpu_id: gpu_id.clone(),
  824:             });
  825:         }
  826:     }
  827:     Ok(())
  828: }
  829: 
  830: pub(crate) fn fetch_node_reservation(
  831:     connection: &Connection,
  832:     node_id: &str,
  833: ) -> Result<Option<StoredNodeReservation>, StagingStoreError> {
  834:     let raw = connection
  835:         .query_row(
  836:             "SELECT node_id, job_id, attempt_id, inventory_revision, reserved_at_unix_ms
  837:              FROM coordinator_node_reservations WHERE node_id = ?1",
  838:             rusqlite::params![node_id],
  839:             |row| {
  840:                 Ok((
  841:                     row.get::<_, String>(0)?,
  842:                     row.get::<_, String>(1)?,
  843:                     row.get::<_, String>(2)?,
  844:                     row.get::<_, Vec<u8>>(3)?,
  845:                     row.get::<_, Vec<u8>>(4)?,
  846:                 ))
  847:             },
  848:         )
  849:         .optional()
  850:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  851:     let Some((node_id, job_id, attempt_id, revision, reserved_at)) = raw else {
  852:         return Ok(None);
  853:     };
  854:     if node_id.trim().is_empty() || job_id.trim().is_empty() || attempt_id.trim().is_empty() {
  855:         return Err(StagingStoreError::CorruptData(
  856:             "node reservation owner identity is blank".into(),
  857:         ));
  858:     }
  859:     let selected_gpu_ids = fetch_gpu_binding(connection, &node_id)?;
  860:     Ok(Some(StoredNodeReservation {
  861:         node_id,
  862:         job_id,
  863:         attempt_id,
  864:         inventory_revision: decode_u64(&revision, "reservation inventory_revision")?,
  865:         reserved_at_unix_ms: decode_u64(&reserved_at, "reservation reserved_at_unix_ms")?,
  866:         selected_gpu_ids,
  867:     }))
  868: }
  869: 
  870: fn fetch_gpu_binding(
  871:     connection: &Connection,
  872:     node_id: &str,
  873: ) -> Result<Vec<String>, StagingStoreError> {
  874:     let mut statement = connection
  875:         .prepare(
  876:             "SELECT gpu_id, ordinal FROM coordinator_node_reservation_gpus
  877:              WHERE node_id = ?1 ORDER BY ordinal",
  878:         )
  879:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  880:     let rows = statement
  881:         .query_map(rusqlite::params![node_id], |row| {
  882:             Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, i64>(1)?))
  883:         })
  884:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?
  885:         .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
  886:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  887:     if rows.is_empty() {
  888:         return Err(StagingStoreError::CorruptData(
  889:             "node reservation has no selected GPU binding".into(),
  890:         ));
  891:     }
  892:     let mut selected_gpu_ids = Vec::with_capacity(rows.len());
  893:     for (expected_ordinal, (gpu_id, ordinal)) in rows.into_iter().enumerate() {
  894:         if ordinal != expected_ordinal as i64 {
  895:             return Err(StagingStoreError::CorruptData(
  896:                 "node reservation GPU ordinals are not contiguous".into(),
  897:             ));
  898:         }
  899:         selected_gpu_ids.push(gpu_id);
  900:     }
  901:     validate_selected_gpu_ids(&selected_gpu_ids).map_err(|_| {
  902:         StagingStoreError::CorruptData(
  903:             "node reservation selected GPU binding is not canonical".into(),
  904:         )
  905:     })?;
  906:     Ok(selected_gpu_ids)
  907: }
  908: 
  909: fn insert_node_reservation(
  910:     connection: &Connection,
  911:     reservation: &StoredNodeReservation,
  912: ) -> Result<(), StagingStoreError> {
  913:     connection
  914:         .execute(
  915:             "INSERT INTO coordinator_node_reservations(
  916:                 node_id, job_id, attempt_id, inventory_revision, reserved_at_unix_ms
  917:              ) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5)",
  918:             rusqlite::params![
  919:                 reservation.node_id,
  920:                 reservation.job_id,
  921:                 reservation.attempt_id,
  922:                 encode_u64(reservation.inventory_revision),
  923:                 encode_u64(reservation.reserved_at_unix_ms),
  924:             ],
  925:         )
  926:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  927:     Ok(())
  928: }
  929: 
  930: fn insert_gpu_binding(
  931:     connection: &Connection,
  932:     reservation: &StoredNodeReservation,
  933: ) -> Result<(), StagingStoreError> {
  934:     for (ordinal, gpu_id) in reservation.selected_gpu_ids.iter().enumerate() {
  935:         connection
  936:             .execute(
  937:                 "INSERT INTO coordinator_node_reservation_gpus(node_id, gpu_id, ordinal)
  938:                  VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)",
  939:                 rusqlite::params![reservation.node_id, gpu_id, ordinal as i64],
  940:             )
  941:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
  942:     }
  943:     Ok(())
  944: }
  945: 
  946: fn validate_replayed_reservation(
  947:     reservation: &StoredNodeReservation,
  948:     request: &StageQueuedRequest,
  949:     expected_inventory_revision: u64,
  950: ) -> Result<(), StagingStoreError> {
  951:     if reservation.node_id != request.node_id
  952:         || reservation.job_id != request.job_id
  953:         || reservation.attempt_id != request.attempt_id
  954:         || reservation.inventory_revision != expected_inventory_revision
  955:         || reservation.reserved_at_unix_ms != request.issued_at_unix_ms
  956:         || reservation.selected_gpu_ids != request.selected_gpu_ids
  957:     {
  958:         return Err(StagingStoreError::CorruptData(
  959:             "reserved staging operation result is inconsistent".into(),
  960:         ));
  961:     }
  962:     Ok(())
  963: }
  964: 
  965: fn load_result(
  966:     connection: &Connection,
  967:     request: &StageQueuedRequest,
  968:     epoch: u64,
  969: ) -> Result<StageQueuedResult, StagingStoreError> {
  970:     let job = job_store::fetch_job(connection, &request.job_id)
  971:         .map_err(map_job_error)?
  972:         .ok_or_else(|| StagingStoreError::CorruptData("operation points to missing Job".into()))?;
  973:     let attempt = fetch_attempt(connection, &request.attempt_id)?.ok_or_else(|| {
  974:         StagingStoreError::CorruptData("operation points to missing Attempt".into())
  975:     })?;
  976:     let current_lease = lease_store::fetch_lease(connection, &request.lease_id)
  977:         .map_err(map_lease_error)?
  978:         .ok_or_else(|| {
  979:             StagingStoreError::CorruptData("operation points to missing Lease".into())
  980:         })?;
  981:     let original_lease = request.to_lease(epoch);
  982:     if job.state != JobState::Staging
  983:         || job.staging_at_unix_ms != Some(request.issued_at_unix_ms)
  984:         || attempt.job_id != request.job_id
  985:         || attempt.lease_id != request.lease_id
  986:         || attempt.node_ids != [request.node_id.clone()]
  987:         || attempt.fence_epoch != epoch
  988:         || attempt.created_at_unix_ms != request.issued_at_unix_ms
  989:         || current_lease.lease_id != original_lease.lease_id
  990:         || current_lease.job_id != original_lease.job_id
  991:         || current_lease.attempt_id != original_lease.attempt_id
  992:         || current_lease.holder_node_id != original_lease.holder_node_id
  993:         || current_lease.fence_epoch != original_lease.fence_epoch
  994:         || current_lease.issuing_coordinator_id != original_lease.issuing_coordinator_id
  995:         || current_lease.coordinator_term != original_lease.coordinator_term
  996:         || current_lease.issued_at_unix_ms != original_lease.issued_at_unix_ms
  997:         || current_lease.max_total_duration_seconds != original_lease.max_total_duration_seconds
  998:         || read_counter(connection)?.is_none_or(|counter| counter < epoch)
  999:     {
 1000:         return Err(StagingStoreError::CorruptData(
 1001:             "staging operation result is inconsistent".into(),
 1002:         ));
 1003:     }
 1004:     Ok(StageQueuedResult {
 1005:         job,
 1006:         attempt,
 1007:         lease: original_lease,
 1008:         created: false,
 1009:     })
 1010: }
 1011: 
 1012: fn encode_u64(value: u64) -> Vec<u8> {
 1013:     value.to_be_bytes().to_vec()
 1014: }
 1015: 
 1016: fn decode_u64(bytes: &[u8], field: &str) -> Result<u64, StagingStoreError> {
 1017:     let value: [u8; 8] = bytes.try_into().map_err(|_| {
 1018:         StagingStoreError::CorruptData(format!("{field} must contain exactly 8 bytes"))
 1019:     })?;
 1020:     Ok(u64::from_be_bytes(value))
 1021: }
 1022: 
 1023: fn map_sql_error(error: SqlError) -> StagingStoreError {
 1024:     match error {
 1025:         SqlError::SqliteFailure(code, _)
 1026:             if matches!(
 1027:                 code.code,
 1028:                 ErrorCode::DatabaseBusy | ErrorCode::DatabaseLocked
 1029:             ) =>
 1030:         {
 1031:             StagingStoreError::LockTimeout
 1032:         }
 1033:         SqlError::SqliteFailure(code, _) => StagingStoreError::Io(code.to_string()),
 1034:         other => StagingStoreError::Io(other.to_string()),
 1035:     }
 1036: }
 1037: 
 1038: fn map_job_error(error: job_store::JobStoreError) -> StagingStoreError {
 1039:     match error {
 1040:         job_store::JobStoreError::LockTimeout => StagingStoreError::LockTimeout,
 1041:         job_store::JobStoreError::CorruptData(message) => StagingStoreError::CorruptData(message),
 1042:         other => StagingStoreError::Io(other.to_string()),
 1043:     }
 1044: }
 1045: 
 1046: fn map_lease_error(error: lease_store::LeaseStoreError) -> StagingStoreError {
 1047:     match error {
 1048:         lease_store::LeaseStoreError::LockTimeout => StagingStoreError::LockTimeout,
 1049:         lease_store::LeaseStoreError::Io(message) => StagingStoreError::CorruptData(message),
 1050:         other => StagingStoreError::Io(other.to_string()),
 1051:     }
 1052: }

 1055: enum TestFault { 1101:                     submitter_device_id: "submitter-1".into(), 1109:         store.start_planning(job_id, 110).unwrap();
 1110:         store.enqueue(job_id, "plan-1", 120).unwrap();
 1111:     }

 1113:     fn prepare_inventory(path: &Path, node_id: &str, revision: u64, seed: u8) {
 1114:         prepare_inventory_gpus(path, node_id, revision, seed, &[format!("gpu-{node_id}")]);
 1115:     }
 1116: 
 1117:     fn prepare_inventory_gpus(
 1118:         path: &Path,
 1119:         node_id: &str,
 1120:         revision: u64,
 1121:         seed: u8,
 1122:         gpu_ids: &[String],
 1123:     ) {
 1124:         let mut store = CoordinatorInventoryStore::open(path).unwrap();
 1125:         if store.get_agent(node_id).unwrap().is_none() {
 1126:             store
 1127:                 .register_agent(&AgentRegistry {
 1128:                     node_id: node_id.into(),
 1129:                     device_id: format!("device-{seed}"),
 1130:                     owner_member_id: "owner-1".into(),
 1131:                     verifying_key: vec![seed; 32],
 1132:                     node_state: None,
 1133:                     risk_state: None,
 1134:                     security_tier: None,
 1135:                     isolation_class: None,
 1136:                     key_protection: None,
 1137:                 })
 1138:                 .unwrap();
 1139:         }
 1140:         store
 1141:             .update_inventory(&AgentInventory {
 1142:                 node_id: node_id.into(),
 1143:                 inventory_revision: revision,
 1144:                 observed_at_unix_ms: revision,
 1145:                 gpus: Some(
 1146:                     gpu_ids
 1147:                         .iter()
 1148:                         .map(|gpu_id| GpuInventory {
 1149:                             gpu_id: gpu_id.clone(),
 1150:                             model: Some("model-a".into()),
 1151:                             healthy: Some(true),
 1152:                             available_vram_bytes: Some(16),
 1153:                         })
 1154:                         .collect(),
 1155:                 ),
 1156:                 available_cpu_cores: Some(8),
 1157:                 available_ram_bytes: Some(64),
 1158:                 available_workspace_bytes: Some(64),
 1159:                 allowed_workload_classes: None,
 1160:                 third_party_workloads_opt_in: None,
 1161:             })
 1162:             .unwrap();
 1163:     }
 1164: 
 1165:     fn request(job_id: &str, operation: u8) -> StageQueuedRequest {
 1166:         StageQueuedRequest {
 1167:             operation_key: [operation; 16],
 1168:             job_id: job_id.into(),
 1169:             attempt_id: format!("attempt-{operation}"),
 1170:             lease_id: format!("lease-{operation}"),
 1171:             node_id: "node-1".into(),
 1172:             selected_gpu_ids: vec!["gpu-node-1".into()],
 1173:             issuing_coordinator_id: "coordinator-1".into(),
 1174:             coordinator_term: 7,
 1175:             issued_at_unix_ms: 200,
 1176:             renew_after_unix_ms: 500,
 1177:             expires_at_unix_ms: 900,
 1178:             max_total_duration_seconds: 1,
 1179:         }
 1180:     }
 1181: 
 1182:     fn assert_queued_and_no_side_effects(
 1183:         store: &CoordinatorStagingStore,
 1184:         request: &StageQueuedRequest,
 1185:     ) {
 1186:         let job = job_store::fetch_job(&store.connection, &request.job_id)
 1187:             .unwrap()
 1188:             .unwrap();
 1189:         assert_eq!(job.state, JobState::Queued);
 1190:         assert_eq!(job.revision, 2);
 1191:         assert_eq!(job.staging_at_unix_ms, None);
 1192:         assert_eq!(store.get_attempt(&request.attempt_id).unwrap(), None);
 1193:         assert_eq!(
 1194:             lease_store::fetch_lease(&store.connection, &request.lease_id).unwrap(),
 1195:             None
 1196:         );
 1197:         assert_eq!(store.fence_epoch().unwrap(), None);
 1198:         assert!(fetch_operation(&store.connection, &request.operation_key)
 1199:             .unwrap()
 1200:             .is_none());
 1201:         assert_eq!(store.get_node_reservation(&request.node_id).unwrap(), None);
 1202:     }

 1204:     #[test]
 1205:     fn stale_or_missing_inventory_fails_before_any_staging_side_effect() {
 1206:         let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
 1207:         let path = temp.path().join("control.sqlite3");
 1208:         prepare_queued(&path, "job-stale", 1);
 1209:         prepare_queued(&path, "job-missing", 2);
 1210:         prepare_inventory(&path, "node-1", 7, 1);
 1211:         let expected = {
 1212:             let mut inventory = CoordinatorInventoryStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 1213:             inventory.pool_snapshot(7).unwrap().candidates[0]
 1214:                 .inventory_revision
 1215:                 .unwrap()
 1216:         };
 1217:         prepare_inventory(&path, "node-1", 8, 1);
 1218:         let mut store = CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 1219: 
 1220:         let stale = request("job-stale", 1);
 1221:         assert_eq!(
 1222:             store.reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&stale, expected),
 1223:             Err(ReservedStageError::InventoryRevisionMismatch {
 1224:                 node_id: "node-1".into(),
 1225:                 expected: 7,
 1226:                 actual: 8,
 1227:             })
 1228:         );
 1229:         assert_queued_and_no_side_effects(&store, &stale);
 1230: 
 1231:         let mut missing = request("job-missing", 2);
 1232:         missing.node_id = "node-missing".into();
 1233:         assert_eq!(
 1234:             store.reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&missing, 1),
 1235:             Err(ReservedStageError::InventoryMissing {
 1236:                 node_id: "node-missing".into(),
 1237:             })
 1238:         );
 1239:         assert_queued_and_no_side_effects(&store, &missing);
 1240:     }

 1242:     #[test]
 1243:     fn sequential_distinct_jobs_cannot_reserve_the_same_node_gpu() {
 1244:         let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
 1245:         let path = temp.path().join("control.sqlite3");
 1246:         prepare_queued(&path, "job-1", 1);
 1247:         prepare_queued(&path, "job-2", 2);
 1248:         prepare_inventory(&path, "node-1", 5, 1);
 1249:         let expected = {
 1250:             let mut inventory = CoordinatorInventoryStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 1251:             inventory.pool_snapshot(5).unwrap().candidates[0]
 1252:                 .inventory_revision
 1253:                 .unwrap()
 1254:         };
 1255:         let first = request("job-1", 1);
 1256:         let second = request("job-2", 2);
 1257:         let mut store = CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 1258: 
 1259:         let winner = store
 1260:             .reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&first, expected)
 1261:             .unwrap();
 1262:         assert_eq!(winner.reservation.job_id, "job-1");
 1263:         assert_eq!(
 1264:             store.reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&second, expected),
 1265:             Err(ReservedStageError::NodeAlreadyReserved {
 1266:                 node_id: "node-1".into(),
 1267:                 owning_job_id: "job-1".into(),
 1268:                 owning_attempt_id: "attempt-1".into(),
 1269:             })
 1270:         );
 1271:         assert_eq!(
 1272:             job_store::fetch_job(&store.connection, "job-2")
 1273:                 .unwrap()
 1274:                 .unwrap()
 1275:                 .state,
 1276:             JobState::Queued
 1277:         );
 1278:         assert_eq!(store.get_attempt("attempt-2").unwrap(), None);
 1279:         assert_eq!(
 1280:             lease_store::fetch_lease(&store.connection, "lease-2").unwrap(),
 1281:             None
 1282:         );
 1283:         assert_eq!(store.fence_epoch().unwrap(), Some(1));
 1284:     }

 1286:     #[test]
 1287:     fn concurrent_distinct_jobs_reserving_one_gpu_commit_exactly_once() {
 1288:         let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
 1289:         let path = temp.path().join("control.sqlite3");
 1290:         prepare_queued(&path, "job-1", 1);
 1291:         prepare_queued(&path, "job-2", 2);
 1292:         prepare_inventory(&path, "node-1", 5, 1);
 1293:         let stores = [
 1294:             CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap(),
 1295:             CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap(),
 1296:         ];
 1297:         let barrier = Arc::new(Barrier::new(2));
 1298:         let handles = stores
 1299:             .into_iter()
 1300:             .enumerate()
 1301:             .map(|(index, mut store)| {
 1302:                 let barrier = Arc::clone(&barrier);
 1303:                 std::thread::spawn(move || {
 1304:                     let request = request(&format!("job-{}", index + 1), index as u8 + 1);
 1305:                     barrier.wait();
 1306:                     store.reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&request, 5)
 1307:                 })
 1308:             })
 1309:             .collect::<Vec<_>>();
 1310:         let results = handles
 1311:             .into_iter()
 1312:             .map(|handle| handle.join().unwrap())
 1313:             .collect::<Vec<_>>();
 1314: 
 1315:         assert_eq!(results.iter().filter(|result| result.is_ok()).count(), 1);
 1316:         assert_eq!(
 1317:             results
 1318:                 .iter()
 1319:                 .filter(|result| matches!(
 1320:                     result,
 1321:                     Err(ReservedStageError::NodeAlreadyReserved { .. })
 1322:                 ))
 1323:                 .count(),
 1324:             1
 1325:         );
 1326:         let winner = results
 1327:             .iter()
 1328:             .find_map(|result| result.as_ref().ok())
 1329:             .unwrap();
 1330:         let loser_job = if winner.reservation.job_id == "job-1" {
 1331:             "job-2"
 1332:         } else {
 1333:             "job-1"
 1334:         };
 1335:         let store = CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 1336:         assert_eq!(
 1337:             store.get_node_reservation("node-1").unwrap(),
 1338:             Some(winner.reservation.clone())
 1339:         );
 1340:         assert_eq!(
 1341:             job_store::fetch_job(&store.connection, loser_job)
 1342:                 .unwrap()
 1343:                 .unwrap()
 1344:                 .state,
 1345:             JobState::Queued
 1346:         );
 1347:         for table in [
 1348:             "coordinator_node_reservations",
 1349:             "coordinator_node_reservation_gpus",
 1350:             "coordinator_attempts",
 1351:             "coordinator_leases",
 1352:             "staging_operation_idempotency",
 1353:         ] {
 1354:             let count: u64 = store
 1355:                 .connection
 1356:                 .query_row(&format!("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {table}"), [], |row| {
 1357:                     row.get(0)
 1358:                 })
 1359:                 .unwrap();
 1360:             assert_eq!(count, 1, "loser must leave no row in {table}");
 1361:         }
 1362:         assert_eq!(store.fence_epoch().unwrap(), Some(1));
 1363:     }
 1364: 
 1365:     #[test]
 1366:     fn distinct_nodes_do_not_share_a_global_reservation_lock() {
 1367:         let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
 1368:         let path = temp.path().join("control.sqlite3");
 1369:         prepare_queued(&path, "job-1", 1);
 1370:         prepare_queued(&path, "job-2", 2);
 1371:         prepare_inventory(&path, "node-1", 5, 1);
 1372:         prepare_inventory(&path, "node-2", u64::MAX, 2);
 1373:         let mut store = CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 1374:         let first = store
 1375:             .reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&request("job-1", 1), 5)
 1376:             .unwrap();
 1377:         let mut second_request = request("job-2", 2);
 1378:         second_request.node_id = "node-2".into();
 1379:         second_request.selected_gpu_ids = vec!["gpu-node-2".into()];
 1380:         let second = store
 1381:             .reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&second_request, u64::MAX)
 1382:             .unwrap();
 1383: 
 1384:         assert_eq!(first.reservation.node_id, "node-1");
 1385:         assert_eq!(second.reservation.node_id, "node-2");
 1386:         assert_eq!(second.reservation.inventory_revision, u64::MAX);
 1387:         assert_eq!(first.stage.lease.fence_epoch, 1);
 1388:         assert_eq!(second.stage.lease.fence_epoch, 2);
 1389:     }

 1391:     #[test]
 1392:     fn exact_reserved_replay_survives_inventory_refresh_but_changed_revision_conflicts() {
 1393:         let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
 1394:         let path = temp.path().join("control.sqlite3");
 1395:         prepare_queued(&path, "job-1", 1);
 1396:         prepare_inventory(&path, "node-1", 5, 1);
 1397:         let request = request("job-1", 1);
 1398:         let mut store = CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 1399:         let first = store
 1400:             .reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&request, 5)
 1401:             .unwrap();
 1402:         drop(store);
 1403:         prepare_inventory(&path, "node-1", 6, 1);
 1404:         let mut store = CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 1405: 
 1406:         let replay = store
 1407:             .reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&request, 5)
 1408:             .unwrap();
 1409:         assert!(!replay.stage.created);
 1410:         assert_eq!(replay.reservation, first.reservation);
 1411:         assert_eq!(
 1412:             replay.stage.lease.fence_epoch,
 1413:             first.stage.lease.fence_epoch
 1414:         );
 1415:         assert_eq!(
 1416:             store.reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&request, 6),
 1417:             Err(ReservedStageError::Staging(
 1418:                 StagingStoreError::OperationConflict
 1419:             ))
 1420:         );
 1421:         assert_eq!(store.fence_epoch().unwrap(), Some(1));
 1422:     }

 1504:                     ))
 1505:                 ),

 1529:     #[test]
 1530:     fn selected_gpu_binding_is_in_operation_payload_and_replay_returns_original() {
 1531:         let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
 1532:         let path = temp.path().join("control.sqlite3");
 1533:         prepare_queued(&path, "job-1", 1);
 1534:         prepare_inventory_gpus(&path, "node-1", 5, 1, &["gpu-a".into(), "gpu-b".into()]);
 1535:         let mut request = request("job-1", 1);
 1536:         request.selected_gpu_ids = vec!["gpu-a".into()];
 1537:         let mut store = CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 1538: 
 1539:         let first = store
 1540:             .reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&request, 5)
 1541:             .unwrap();
 1542:         let replay = store
 1543:             .reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&request, 5)
 1544:             .unwrap();
 1545:         assert!(!replay.stage.created);
 1546:         assert_eq!(replay.reservation, first.reservation);
 1547: 
 1548:         let mut changed = request.clone();
 1549:         changed.selected_gpu_ids = vec!["gpu-b".into()];
 1550:         assert_eq!(
 1551:             store.reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&changed, 5),
 1552:             Err(ReservedStageError::Staging(
 1553:                 StagingStoreError::OperationConflict
 1554:             ))
 1555:         );
 1556:         assert_eq!(
 1557:             store.get_node_reservation("node-1").unwrap(),
 1558:             Some(first.reservation)
 1559:         );
 1560:         assert_eq!(store.fence_epoch().unwrap(), Some(1));
 1561:     }

 1564:     fn missing_blank_noncanonical_or_gapped_stored_gpu_binding_fails_closed() {
 1565:         for corruption in ["missing", "blank", "noncanonical", "gapped"] {
 1566:             let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
 1567:             let path = temp.path().join("control.sqlite3");
 1568:             prepare_queued(&path, "job-1", 1);
 1569:             prepare_inventory_gpus(&path, "node-1", 5, 1, &["gpu-a".into(), "gpu-b".into()]);
 1570:             let mut request = request("job-1", 1);
 1571:             request.selected_gpu_ids = vec!["gpu-a".into(), "gpu-b".into()];
 1572:             let mut store = CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 1573:             store 1636:         let mut request = request("job-1", 1); 1645:         let mut reopened = CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 1646:         assert_eq!(
 1647:             reopened.get_node_reservation("node-1").unwrap(),
 1648:             Some(issued.reservation.clone())
 1649:         );
 1650:         assert_eq!(issued.reservation.inventory_revision, 0);
 1651:         assert_eq!(
 1652:             issued.reservation.selected_gpu_ids,
 1653:             ["gpu-a".to_owned(), "gpu-z".to_owned()]
 1654:         );
 1655:         let replay = reopened
 1656:             .reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&request, 0)
 1657:             .unwrap();
 1658:         assert!(!replay.stage.created);

 1663:     fn corrupt_inventory_and_reservation_revision_or_owner_fail_closed() {
 1664:         for corruption in [
 1665:             "inventory-revision",
 1666:             "reservation-revision",
 1667:             "reservation-owner",
 1668:         ] {
 1669:             let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
 1670:             let path = temp.path().join("control.sqlite3");
 1671:             prepare_queued(&path, "job-1", 1);
 1672:             prepare_inventory(&path, "node-1", 5, 1);
 1673:             let request = request("job-1", 1);
 1674:             let mut store = CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 1675:             if corruption == "inventory-revision" {
 1676:                 store
 1677:                     .connection
 1678:                     .execute(
 1679:                         "UPDATE coordinator_agent_inventory SET inventory_revision = x'01' WHERE node_id = 'node-1'",
 1680:                         [],
 1681:                     )
 1682:                     .unwrap();
 1683:                 assert!(matches!(
 1684:                     store.reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&request, 5),
 1685:                     Err(ReservedStageError::Staging(StagingStoreError::CorruptData(
 1686:                         _
 1687:                     )))
 1688:                 ));
 1689:                 assert_queued_and_no_side_effects(&store, &request);
 1690:                 continue;
 1691:             }
 1692: 
 1693:             store
 1694:                 .reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&request, 5)
 1695:                 .unwrap();
 1696:             if corruption == "reservation-revision" {
 1697:                 store
 1698:                     .connection
 1699:                     .execute(
 1700:                         "UPDATE coordinator_node_reservations SET inventory_revision = x'01' WHERE node_id = 'node-1'",
 1701:                         [],
 1702:                     )
 1703:                     .unwrap();
 1704:             } else {
 1705:                 store
 1706:                     .connection
 1707:                     .execute_batch("PRAGMA foreign_keys = OFF;")
 1708:                     .unwrap();
 1709:                 store
 1710:                     .connection
 1711:                     .execute(
 1712:                         "UPDATE coordinator_node_reservations SET job_id = ' ' WHERE node_id = 'node-1'",
 1713:                         [],
 1714:                     )
 1715:                     .unwrap();
 1716:                 store
 1717:                     .connection
 1718:                     .execute_batch("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;")
 1719:                     .unwrap();
 1720:             }
 1721:             assert!(matches!(
 1722:                 store.reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(&request, 5),
 1723:                 Err(ReservedStageError::Staging(StagingStoreError::CorruptData(
 1724:                     _
 1725:                 )))
 1726:             ));
 1727:             assert_eq!(store.fence_epoch().unwrap(), Some(1));
 1728:         }
 1729:     }

 1731:     #[test]
 1732:     fn success_is_consistent_and_identical_retry_does_not_consume_epoch() {
 1733:         let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
 1734:         let path = temp.path().join("control.sqlite3");
 1735:         prepare_queued(&path, "job-1", 1);
 1736:         let mut request = request("job-1", 1);
 1737:         request.selected_gpu_ids.clear();
 1738:         let mut store = CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 1739: 
 1740:         let first = store.stage_queued_with_lease(&request).unwrap();
 1741:         assert!(first.created);
 1742:         assert_eq!(first.job.state, JobState::Staging);
 1743:         assert_eq!(
 1744:             first.job.staging_at_unix_ms,
 1745:             Some(request.issued_at_unix_ms)
 1746:         );
 1747:         assert_eq!(first.job.revision, 3);
 1748:         assert_eq!(first.attempt.state, AttemptState::Created);
 1749:         assert_eq!(first.attempt.node_ids, [request.node_id.clone()]);
 1750:         assert_eq!(first.attempt.job_id, request.job_id);
 1751:         assert_eq!(first.attempt.attempt_id, request.attempt_id);
 1752:         assert_eq!(first.attempt.lease_id, request.lease_id);
 1753:         assert_eq!(first.attempt.fence_epoch, first.lease.fence_epoch);
 1754:         assert_eq!(first.lease.job_id, first.job.job_id);
 1755:         assert_eq!(first.lease.attempt_id, first.attempt.attempt_id);
 1756:         assert_eq!(first.lease.holder_node_id, first.attempt.node_ids[0]);
 1757:         assert_eq!(store.fence_epoch().unwrap(), Some(first.lease.fence_epoch));
 1758: 
 1759:         let retry = store.stage_queued_with_lease(&request).unwrap();
 1760:         assert!(!retry.created);
 1761:         assert_eq!(retry.lease.fence_epoch, first.lease.fence_epoch);
 1762:         assert_eq!(store.fence_epoch().unwrap(), Some(first.lease.fence_epoch));
 1763:     }

 1765:     #[test]
 1766:     fn every_injected_write_failure_rolls_back_job_attempt_lease_counter_and_operation() {
 1767:         for (index, fault) in [
 1768:             TestFault::AfterAttemptInsert,
 1769:             TestFault::AfterLeaseInsert,
 1770:             TestFault::BeforeJobUpdate,
 1771:         ]
 1772:         .into_iter()
 1773:         .enumerate()
 1774:         {
 1775:             let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
 1776:             let path = temp.path().join("control.sqlite3");
 1777:             let job_id = format!("job-{index}");
 1778:             prepare_queued(&path, &job_id, index as u8 + 1);
 1779:             let request = request(&job_id, index as u8 + 1);
 1780:             let mut store = CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 1781:             assert!(matches!(
 1782:                 store.stage(&request, Some(fault)),
 1783:                 Err(StagingStoreError::InjectedFailure(_))
 1784:             ));
 1785:             assert_queued_and_no_side_effects(&store, &request);
 1786: 
 1787:             let result = store.stage_queued_with_lease(&request).unwrap();
 1788:             assert_eq!(
 1789:                 result.lease.fence_epoch, 1,
 1790:                 "rollback must not consume epoch"
 1791:             );
 1792:         }
 1793:     }

 2062:     #[test]
 2063:     fn reopen_preserves_all_state_and_standalone_stores_read_and_mutate_the_lease() {
 2064:         let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
 2065:         let path = temp.path().join("control.sqlite3");
 2066:         prepare_queued(&path, "job-1", 1);
 2067:         let request = request("job-1", 1);
 2068:         let issued = {
 2069:             let mut store = CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 2070:             store.stage_queued_with_lease(&request).unwrap()
 2071:         };
 2072:         let reopened = CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 2073:         assert_eq!(
 2074:             reopened.fence_epoch().unwrap(),
 2075:             Some(issued.lease.fence_epoch)
 2076:         );
 2077:         assert_eq!(
 2078:             reopened.get_attempt(&request.attempt_id).unwrap(),
 2079:             Some(issued.attempt.clone())
 2080:         );
 2081:         drop(reopened);
 2082: 
 2083:         let jobs = CoordinatorJobStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 2084:         assert_eq!(jobs.get(&request.job_id).unwrap().unwrap(), issued.job);
 2085:         drop(jobs);
 2086:         let mut leases = CoordinatorLeaseStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 2087:         assert_eq!(
 2088:             leases.get(&request.lease_id).unwrap(),
 2089:             Some(issued.lease.clone())
 2090:         );
 2091:         let renewed = leases.renew_existing(&request.lease_id, 950, 600).unwrap();
 2092:         assert_eq!(renewed.fence_epoch, issued.lease.fence_epoch);
 2093:         leases.mark_revoked(&request.lease_id, 700).unwrap();
 2094:         drop(leases);
 2095:         let leases = CoordinatorLeaseStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 2096:         assert_eq!(
 2097:             leases
 2098:                 .get(&request.lease_id)
 2099:                 .unwrap()
 2100:                 .unwrap()
 2101:                 .revoked_at_unix_ms,
 2102:             Some(700)
 2103:         );
 2104:         drop(leases);
 2105: 
 2106:         let mut staging = CoordinatorStagingStore::open(&path).unwrap();
 2107:         let retry = staging.stage_queued_with_lease(&request).unwrap();
 2108:         assert!(!retry.created);
 2109:         assert_eq!(
 2110:             retry.lease, issued.lease,
 2111:             "retry returns the original operation result"
 2112:         );
 2113:     }

"//! Single-node local atomic `QUEUED -> STAGING` storage kernel.\n//!\n//!
